### build 25 (שוחזר אחרי אובדן הסביבה, 22.10) — the lamb pen: inspecting for blemishes by torchlight (21.10, reported ≈ 10:17; docs/specs/dir-hatlaim-he.md)
- **What:** all 7 parts, in `pen25.js` and day33.js. (1) **The lamb as a lot task:** it is built into `POOL23`; morning 3 is the lamb; from day 4 it is in the double lot, never two days running; the pen's gate (`gate25`, in `gateAny`) holds the day's clock, the fire and the stations before dawn; when he draws the lamb the deshen is already done and the pyre is the other kohanim's, lit when he approves; when he draws another task, a kohen with a torch checks the lamb at the pen door and leads it out at dawn; the walk is by the stick along the strong path (taps on the bar, ring or card only show the way); the inspection starts at his place (`p25Step`). (2) **The inspection:** the left hand off; the screen dark with one warm hole and a torch light; the lamb alive on the animal rig (`sheepRig`; breathing, head, ear and leg moves); the right thumb aims the beam with a relative drag ×1.6; a spot under the beam gets a focus ring and a 14 ms buzz; a right tap zooms in 0.4 s; dirt is brushed off by a swipe; a blemish is shown clearly. (3) **The two stamps** — "תמים" (gold-copper) and "פסול" (dark wood), 92 px, inside the right half: "פסול" sets the lamb aside and a ≥ 64 px button brings a new one (at most 2 rejections a morning); "תמים" on a whole lamb — a gold seal "תמים לקרבן", a copper bell, the door opens, a kohen leads it toward the rings, the task is done (`p25done`); a false approval (`p25stamp`) costs a 3.2 s delay ("עיכוב!") and a replacement lamb; his first lamb is easier to read (`lamb25`). (4) **48 blemishes** — 6 kinds (יבלת/בליטה, סדק/חריץ, כתם/עכירות אישון, פצע/חתך, קרע/חסר בבשר, נפיחות/עיוות) × 8 places (אוזן ימין/שמאל, עין ימין/שמאל, טלף קדמית/אחורית, גב, בטן): each lamb whole or with one blemish, plus 0–2 dirt spots, seeded by the day; the decals drawn in code, browns and greys, no red; labelled "המחשה" with the Rambam line. (5) **The dawn clock** in the pen's window — no fail, it only scores. (6) **Sounds and buzzes:** five new synthesized sounds in audio.js (torch, hooves, bleat, stamp, bell). (7) **Mastery and coins:** `mastery23('lamb')` feeds the roles book, the record and the summary line; points 120, +20, −40, −20; coins 3 / 2 / 1 by quality. Sources shown as drafts ("טיוטה"): "מערבית דרומית, היא היתה לשכת טלאי קרבן" (Middot 1:6); "בודקין אותו לאור האבוקות" (Tamid 3:4); "צאו והביאו טלה מלשכת הטלאים" (Tamid 3:3); "כל מום משלשה ושבעים מום המנויין בבהמה פוסלין אותה מן הקרבן" (Rambam, Isurei Mizbe'ach 2:10).
- **Numbers:** `dist/temple3d.html` 5,372,398 B (+30,678 over build 24's 5,341,720; the budget was 30,720; 919,058 B under the 6 MiB cap); **686 tests (676 pass, 10 skipped, 0 fail)** — `test/build25.test.mjs` adds 10, including the morning ≤ 40 s for a quick hand and ≤ 45 s at a child's pace; build23's test now expects the lamb built. **The morning, autoplayer on the final build (normal / human), laver · lot · walk · task:** day 1 (deshen) 29.4 s mastery 98 / 37.2 s mastery 79; day 2 (pyre) 29.7 s mastery 100 / cut by the run's end; **day 3 (lamb) 3.7 · 3.9 · 12.4 · 8.2 = 28.2 s mastery 100 / 5.1 · 4.7 · 15.9 · 11.0 = 36.7 s mastery 100**; day 4 (double lot, the lamb not offered) 27.0 s mastery 98 / 38.6 s mastery 64. Day 3's lamb, normal: the back wound found at the first look (1 tap), "פסול", a new lamb, "תמים"; his own time 4.4 s, 1 rejection, 0 false approvals, 0 wrong rejections, 0 dirt mistaken, 140 points, 3 coins (human: 6.6 s, 1 rejection, 0 / 0 / 0, 3 coins). Fire, normal: 70–100, 55–100, 64–100, then 65–86; both tamids on time on days 1–3; human: day 2's evening tamid late (the pyre day, the fire never fed) in both series. Coins per day, normal / human: 190 / 68 / 208 and 122 / 52 / 68. **A seeding bug found and fixed before the final runs:** the old hash made the first lamb of days 3–10 whole every time (no blemish for about a week) — `hash25` now mixes harder, day 3's first lamb is an obvious wound on the back, and a test guards the mix (before the fix: the lamb morning 26.6 s normal, 34.6 s human; the human day-2 pyre morning 45.3 s, 0.3 s over the cap — a build-24 issue, 42.9–44.7 s there).
- **Verified by:** node tests; the autoplayer normal (`b25m`) and human (`b25mh`), a fresh 7-minute run plus `--continue` ×2 each, 0 JS errors; a browser probe for the zoom, the light and the camera views; PNGs, each looked at: the dark pen with the beam on the flank, the gold focus ring with its source line, dirt on the back, "הוברש — נקי ✓", a wart on the left ear labelled "המחשה", a long cut across the back ("מום: פצע / חתך · גב"), the two stamps inside the right half, "עיכוב!", the bring button, the seal on the lamb with a kohen leading it out of the open door, the NPC kohen with a torch, the roles book ("בדיקת הטלה 86% · 2 פעמים").
- **What remains:** no false approval or wrong rejection happened in the autoplayer's runs — those paths are checked only by the browser probe and the node tests; the walk is the biggest part of the lamb morning (124 amot ≈ 12 s; moving the inspection spot or the lot's circle closer would shorten it — the owner's call); an autoplayer gap that predates the build (a log fallen on the ramp has no floor path — the normal player stood idle by it 40–75 s in two runs); the sounds and haptics were never heard or felt (headless).
- **Deviations:** the beam uses a 2-D relative drag (the protocol lists only 1-D drags; the pen spec asks to aim the torch); the zoom narrows the lens instead of moving the camera in (the near plane, 1.5 amot, clipped the lamb); blemishes are drawn (the spec supersedes the old kit note that said not to); the inspection place is the pen station's door, and the walk to it is 124 amot, not the 89 first estimated (the porch steps block the west side; a probe sampled a steady 10.5 amot/s); approving the lamb (his or the other kohanim's) also marks the dawn tamid's lamb as checked; the spot sound reuses `near17`; day 3's first lamb is the same for every player (seeded by the day). **A rule lapse reported by the agent:** it ran `node -e "require(probe25.cjs)"` outside `brun`, which probably started a browser outside the queue for a short time (no process was listed or killed; not repeated).

### build 26 (שוחזר אחרי אובדן הסביבה, 22.10) — the Heichal in the morning (21.10, reported ≈ 15:25; docs/specs/heichal-boker-he.md, heichal-he.md, ketoret-he.md written the same morning)
- **What:** all 7 parts. (1) **The second lot** at dawn, after the pre-dawn task, in build 23's circle, reached by the left stick; it reuses pis23's lot (`p23start(k26)`, its own `save.roles26`); the roles are `POOL26` (Yoma 2:3's 13 roles, 2 built); the schedule `P26.open`: morning 1 menorah, 2 inner altar, 3 menorah; from morning 4 never yesterday's role (with two built roles they alternate); the morning holds (the day clock, the fire's drain, the stations) through `gate26L` / `gate26` in `gateAny`; the other kohanim visibly do the unbuilt roles (the shochet at the rings, the zorek who throws only after the five lamps, four limb carriers); the roles book shows those 11 as "כהנים אחרים — בקרוב". (2) **The great gate:** a right swipe across draws the bolt, a right hold turns the key (lifted, it turns back); the deep echoing sound `gate26`; the Jericho line once (`save.jer26`); the NPC slaughter starts only from the gate's open time; he walks in by the left stick on the strong path — the walkable area `IN26` / `in26` (world22.js), flow31's `freeNow` plans by it; no auto-walk. (3) **The Heichal**, procedural, no models: sanctuary.js's room; the menorah south, the golden altar in the middle, the table north (as places.js; checked against Yoma 33b on Sefaria and by a test); the parochet stays closed — the walkable floor stops an amah short of it (x −136.4); the menorah has round branches (sanctuary's `style:'round'` — no text attributes the branch shape, and a test enforces that); the darkness from a vignette; fake volumetric light (cones, halos, a card on the parochet, the gate's light shaft); the camera automatic, authored views (`camWork26`). (4) **The inner altar:** the teni set before him; seven right-thumb pulls — six handfuls of 15 % and the sweep; a pull too fast scratches the gold (−5 % on the "המחשה" meter); the teni is left inside; the second entry (Tamid 6:1) is always another kohen, seen on screen. (5) **The menorah:** he stands on the stone's second step; per lamp: swipe up (wick out), swipe across (wipe), hold to pour into the 90–100 % band, tap for the new wick (under the band: hold again; over it: it spills and he tries again, free); the two eastern lamps are found lit and left; the rare event (seeded, about one morning in five, never his first): the easternmost lamp is found out and relit by a right-thumb drag from its burning neighbour; the kuz on the second step; the remaining 2 lamps are a HUD line. (6) **Juice:** eight synthesized sounds (gate, bolt, echoing step, handful, scrape, pour tone 300 → 800 Hz, gold bell, wick), a short buzz at the band, a sharp one on the new wick, the light grows with each lamp; the Heichal's deep silence not implemented. (7) **Mastery, coins, roles book:** `mastery23('inner'|'menorah')`, `done26` (coins 3 / 2 / 1 by quality); "השיא שלך" at the role's start, "שיא חדש!" at the end.
- **Numbers:** **697 tests (687 pass, 10 skipped, 0 fail)** — `test/build26.test.mjs` 11 tests, including a Divine-Name scan by `String.fromCharCode` (one full-suite run under concurrent browser load failed cam28's "never a cut" once; it passed on the following runs); `dist/temple3d.html` **5,412,627 B** (+40,229 over 5,372,398; budget 61,440; 878,829 B under the cap); out/index.html titled "משמרת 45 (בנייה 26) — בית המקדש השני". **The morning (autoplayer, 18 runs, 0 JS errors), normal:** day 1 (deshen, menorah) the dawn role from dawn 42.9 s, from the second lot 34.8 s, the whole morning 72.5 s, mastery 94 (breakdown: back to the circle 8.1, lot 3.8, to the gate 3.9, gate 2.4, walk in 4.1, lamps 20.6; pre-dawn 29.6); day 2 (pyre, inner altar) 31.2 s / 19.3 s / 61.1 s, mastery 99; day 3 (lamb, menorah) 49.9 s / 34.1 s / 78.0 s, mastery 95, a new record. **Human (child pace):** menorah day 1 60.3 s / 50.0 s / 95.0 s, mastery 60; inner altar day 2 38.0 s / 25.3 s / 81.4 s, mastery 77; menorah day 3 66.0 s / 46.8 s / 104.3 s, mastery 72. Coins per role: 3 normal, 2 human. Fire: day 1 70–100, day 2 50–100, day 3 65–100; both tamids on time. Day coins 180 / 63 / 223.
- **Verified by:** node tests; 18 autoplayer runs through `brun`; 14 PNGs, each opened: the bolt in the Ulam ("החליקו לרוחב"), "השער הגדול נפתח!", the dark Heichal from the doorway (lit menorah south, golden altar, table north, parochet closed), the two eastern lamps lit with a ring on the next, the pour meter at 96 % inside the gold band, three flames after the first lamp, the found-out lamp ("גררו את האש"), handfuls into the teni, "לאט! שריטה בזהב", "הטני בפנים — ויצא", the kuz, the court at dawn, four kohanim carrying limbs, the roles book (menorah 100 %, inner altar 86 %, 11 roles "בקרוב"). **Bugs found and fixed in the runs:** the way out of the Heichal (flow31's planner did not know the chambers' block north of the altar, stages.js `'lishka'`, x −54 … −22; the human autoplayer stood ~100 s against its west face — the strong path now goes round it, `LISHKA26`, with a test); the gate's reach (`P26.reach` 2.4 left a dead zone where the strong path vanishes at 3 amot — raised to 3.2, a test); the autoplayer's pour now runs on the game clock.
- **What remains:** **The menorah misses the target** (≤ 40 s): 60–66 s at the human pace (the inner altar meets it). The costs: the walk back to the circle (8–19 s) and the five lamps' 20 gestures (30–33 s human); levers for the owner: hold the second lot where he stands or at the Ulam steps; fewer gestures after the first lamp; a faster pour; the menorah's mastery time scale (14 → 34 s) looks harsh at child pace. The Heichal's deep silence not built. Sounds and buzzes never heard or felt; the darkness tuned on SwiftShader frames, not on the owner's phone. A pause bug found by reading the code, not tested (a pause mid-pour could carry the hold on and spill the lamp, free retry) — fixed in 26b.
- **Deviations:** (1) the gate is opened from the Ulam side (the Mishnah: through the northern wicket, from inside); (2) the second lot happens at dawn in the circle (Tamid 3:1: right after the first lot, in Lishkat HaGazit); (3) from morning 4 the second lot is a single card (only 2 roles built); (4) the NPC always does the second entry even when his own inner altar took ≤ 20 s; (5) he stands on the stone's second step, not the top (the top sits under the eastern branch in the model); (6) the parochet is sanctuary.js's single visual, not the Second Temple's two curtains; (7) the tended lamps relight automatically at the tap — only the found-out lamp needs the drag, and the western lamp's own rule is not built; (8) the remaining 2 lamps are a HUD line only; (9) while an authored camera view is on screen the stick's direction follows that view (a camera.js change). **Rule lapse reported:** one probe chain launched with a shell `&` instead of `run_in_background` (each browser inside it still went through `brun`, one at a time).

### build 26b (שוחזר אחרי אובדן הסביבה, 22.10) — the Heichal morning within its time (21.10, reported ≈ 19:01)
- **What:** 8 parts. (1) **The lot where he stands:** the circle forms round him (`free26` / `circle26`; the kohanim and the ממונה walk in; the count animation kept); on the altar's top the card says "רדו מן המזבח" and the lot happens at the ramp's foot; chosen over "at the Ulam steps" because he knows his role at once — measured honestly: the old circle by the laver was already on the way, the change saves about 9 s only on the lamb morning. (2) **Lamps 2–5 in 3 gestures:** one swipe up removes the wick and wipes the lamp (both animate); lamp 1 keeps all 4 gestures; the band, the top-up and the free retry unchanged. (3) **A faster pour:** the fast phase 1.1 → 1.5 per second; the band is hit at 1.06–1.35 s instead of 1.22–1.52 (its width in time unchanged, 0.29 s); the rising tone kept. (4) **Mastery scale** of the menorah 14 → 34 became 13 → 39: a quick hand gets 98, a child 78–82 (was 60–72). (5) **Camera:** from his right side, about 3.2 amot from the lamp; the menorah ≈ 275 px wide (was 159), his head at the left edge; clearance lamp–head 220–459 px for lamps 1–5 (was 110–131 for 1–3); the active lamp's ring is now thin gold dots. (6) **Pause bug:** any pause releases the held pour or key, judges nothing, and refuses a new hold while paused (node test; the browser probe confirmed: the fill stayed 0.53 after resuming, then a fresh press poured into the band). (7) **The Heichal's silence:** the music fades to 20 % over about 1.2 s past the doorway and returns on the way out (probed 0.2 inside, 1.0 outside). **Found:** the shift's sound kit is `src/avodah/sound.js` (`src/main.js` passes the game no sound kit), and that kit lacks `gate26`, `step26`, `pour26`, `bell26`, `chord17`, `ding17` and the rest — **every day sound since build 17 was silent in the game**; not switched in 26b (about 60 sounds nobody had heard would have turned on at once — the owner's call; done in 26c). (8) **b26-npc:** that shot came from build 26's own probe pointing the camera at the court; the game never shows the outside while a right-hand instruction is on screen (node test) — nothing to fix. Also fixed: the circle inside the chambers' block (a wrong rectangle); the exit off the Ulam steps (the path's floor and the exit rule disagreed by 0.35 amah and trapped him 18+ s — the steps' foot corners now allow an extra amah, a test failed before the fix); the autoplayer re-presses the pour after a pause and follows the gold path itself when stuck.
- **Numbers:** **705 tests (695 pass, 10 skipped, 0 fail)** — `test/build26b.test.mjs` 8 tests, build26's updated; `dist/temple3d.html` **5,415,278 B** (+2,651 over 5,412,627; ≈ 876 KB under the cap); out/index.html titled "משמרת 45 (בנייה 26ב) — בית המקדש השני". **Seconds from the dawn, build 26 → 26b (normal / human):** deshen + menorah 42.9 → **38.9** / 60.3 → **54.1** (whole morning 72.5 → 69.2 / 95.0 → 93.6); pyre + inner altar 31.2 → 29.8 / 38.0 → 38.4 (unchanged within noise: the walk is the same; whole morning 61.1 → 60.0 / 81.4 → 85.2); lamb + menorah 49.9 → **38.8** / 66.0 → **50.3** (whole morning 78.0 → 68.1 / 104.3 → 86.9). Pre-dawn phase: normal 29.3–30.3 s, human 36.6–46.8 s. Other human samples: menorah 51.5–57.8, inner 40.3–42.5.
- **Verified by:** node tests; six final autoplayer runs (`b26bN`, `b26bH`), 0 JS errors; PNGs, each looked at: the circle formed round him by the heap, "הממונה: 10!", lamp 1 with the big menorah and his face clear of the lamp, lamp 3 (the one-swipe hint), lamp 5 (the western), the pour gauge at 96 %, lamp 3 lit from its neighbour, the dragged flame (his head ≈ 90 px from lamp 6 — clear but crowded), the inner altar, and the old view for comparison.
- **What remains:** **The main target was missed:** a child's pace on the menorah is 50–54 s from the dawn on the final build (50–58 across runs; down from 60–66; a quick hand 38.8–40.1 s) against ≤ 40 s — about 23 s pass before the first lamp, mostly the 11.5–12 s walk to the gate (84–94 amot), and the five lamps take another 24–29 s. **Levers not pulled** (each changes a source step or the pace): the partner tends two of the lamps (~8 s); a faster morning run (~2.5 s); fold the "lit" pause and the kuz tap into the last tap (~2 s); a wider band (~3 s). The silent kit (done in 26c). Once the human autoplayer stood ≈ 70 s at the gate doorway's south jamb on the way out (suspected: the autoplayer's stick direction against the camera's set view; not reproduced); autoplayer stalls later in the day by the pen door and on the ramp, outside this build's routes.
- **Deviations:** the lot at the ramp's foot instead of Lishkat HaGazit (Tamid 3:1) — as in 26; "reach the band in ≈ 1.2–1.5 s" was already the old band, so it was read as the release landing around 1.2 s. **Rule lapses reported:** a `brun` run launched with a shell `&`; one `pgrep` in a wait loop (it lists processes); no process was killed.

### build 26c (שוחזר אחרי אובדן הסביבה, 22.10) — the day's sounds now play in Phase B (21.10, reported ≈ 19:37)
- **What:** **One AudioContext, one unlock, one mute.** `src/avodah/sound.js` `createSound({ storage, more })` now also builds audio.js's kit, `createAudio({ storage: null, trim: TRIM26C, declick: true })`, on the same context; routing (`play` / `playOne`): sound.js plays its own names, otherwise audio.js does (where both kits have a name, sound.js's wins, unchanged); the kit is callable with `has`, `names()`, `own`, `choir`, `rush20`, `duck` and `context`; the mute toggle sets both kits and writes only `bh_avodah_mute` (Phase A's `bh_mute` is never written from Phase B; a saved mute from either key mutes both); the first gesture unlocks both kits; the context is suspended while the page is hidden (the choir is a held sound); `src/main.js`: the music now shares Phase B's context. **Phase A is unchanged** (`createAudio`'s new options `trim` and `declick` are passed only by Phase B; audio.js's beds go through a new gain bus at 1, `duck`). **The Heichal's ducking:** `mute26` now calls `o.sfx.duck(k)` — it lowers the beds bus (ambience, running water, the Levites' choir): the choir's RMS400 −37.2 → −50.5 dBFS inside; `step26` goes straight to the master (inside: the steps −44.4 against a ducked bed −52.3, phone-weighted — the echo is heard). **A one-sample click on every sound's start** (a gain node at 1 until its first scheduled event, a source starting one frame earlier) fixed in the day kit (`declick`) and in sound.js's `env` / `tone`: the salt grains' peak −3.5 → −20.4 dBFS. **Levels:** every name rendered through an OfflineAudioContext in the headless browser; "phone" = RMS100 after a 300 Hz high-pass; median of the 63 played one-shots −31.0 → **−32.2 dBFS**; trims `TRIM26C`: bell25 ×0.25, perfect ×0.27, starPop ×0.27, chord17 ×0.36, bell26 ×0.38, ding17 ×0.39, trumpet17 ×0.47, blast21 ×0.54, near17 ×0.56, wbell20 ×0.68, late17 ×0.77, bell ×0.9, gate26 ×0.46, stamp25 ×0.46, drum23 ×0.72, alarm17 ×0.73, knock22 ×0.83, warn17 ×0.89, raised: tap ×2.92, drip ×2.63, bleat25 ×1.64; repeating: hoof25 ×0.6 (it had been as loud as the music bed), pour26 ×0.65, the choir at full fire ×0.3 (−28.7 → −39.1; it had stood 10 dB over the music on a phone); unchanged: step26, torch25, salt19, rush20 and sound.js's own names. Every one-shot of the day kit within +5.9 dB of the median on a phone; the loudest Phase B peak −4.4 dBFS; the combined kit's every name peaks ≤ −1.06 dBFS (Phase A's `stamp`, which Phase B never plays). The full table of all 121 names was in the original PROJECT.md entry and in `scratchpad/b26c/table.txt` (both lost).
- **Numbers:** **712 tests (702 pass, 10 skipped, 0 fail)** — `test/build26c.test.mjs`, 7 tests (705 before); `dist/temple3d.html` **5,417,106 B** (+1,828 over 26b, under the 6 MiB cap); out/index.html titled "משמרת 45 (בנייה 26ג) — בית המקדש השני". **The guard test collects 63 literal sound names** from the source of shift/, day/, works/, minigames/, index.js and ui.js (the `snd` / `sfx` / `sfxPlay` / `.sfx.play` / `ctx.sfx` / `this.sfx` calls, both branches of a ternary, the works' `{sfx:'x'}`, the name tables `SWING17.sfx` and fx18's `SND`); each must start a source on a fake WebAudio context, using the kit exactly as index.js builds it. **On the pre-fix source it fails: "43 of 63 names make no voice"** (6 of 7 tests fail): alarm17, bell, bell25, bell26, blast21, bleat25, bolt26, chord17, coin18, crack22, ding17, drip, drum23, err22, fire22, gate26, good, hand26, hoof25, knock22, late17, near17, perfect, pop, pour26, salt19, scrape21, scrape26, soft, spill21, split, stamp25, starPop, step26, tab, tap, torch25, trumpet17, unlock20, warn17, wbell20, whoosh, wick26 — after the fix 7 of 7 pass. **What plays in a day** (autoplayer `b26c`, 7 minutes, normal pace, `--sound`, 0 JS errors, the context running): day 1 — 667 play calls of 36 distinct names, every one made a voice (coin18 165, salt19 95, pour26 71, near17 39, ui 35, step26 29, perfect 26, soft 25, ding17 21, whoosh 20, chord17 19, drum23 16, split 15, …); before the fix only 8 of those 36 names would have sounded (69 of the 667 calls); day 2's first part 234 calls of 29 names, all voiced; the menorah from the dawn 40.1 s, the morning 70.0 s.
- **Verified by:** node tests (the guard fails on 26b's HEAD and passes after); the levels rendered offline in the headless browser via `brun`; an autoplayer run with the counter (`--run b26c --minutes 7 --shots 1 --sound` — the flag only adds the autoplay-policy switch and the counter, because the headless AudioContext never runs on synthetic pointer events); the run's screenshot was not opened (not relevant to sound).
- **What remains:** **Nobody has heard any of this on a phone** — 43 names and two beds had never been heard by the owner or Dror; the levels come from measurement only. **The bass booms are mostly inaudible on a phone speaker** — gate26 (52 → 38 Hz), fire22 (62 Hz), stamp25 (72 Hz), drum23's end (92 Hz), sound.js's thud (70 Hz) and grave (98 Hz): on a phone `grave` sits ≈ 13 dB under the median, so the slaughter tone is close to inaudible; through headphones the gate is 17 dB over the raw median — the fix is a higher partial a phone can play (a sound-design change, not a level change). sound.js's own quiet names kept exactly as the brief said (ui, tick, pour, water, step, crowd, miss: 10–27 dB under the median on a phone). **Phase A's kit has the same click** — left alone (Phase A had to stay unchanged); one line (turn `declick` on) if the owner wants it.
- **Deviations:** fx18's number pop ("+N 🪙") stays off — the combined kit does not expose `pop`, because that is a visual change; sound.js's `grave` still wins over audio.js's, so the slaughter tone does not dip the choir; the choir holds its level under a pause card. **Rule lapse reported:** one `brun` run launched in the foreground instead of `run_in_background` (a short click probe, still through `brun`).

### build 27 — the second lot's two blood roles, "מי שוחט, מי זורק" (22.10; docs/specs/shchita-kabala-he.md, zerikat-dam-he.md, tipul-taleh-he.md; the new module `src/avodah/shift/dam27.js`, the engine in day33.js)
- **What:** all 6 parts. (1) **The lot:** `POOL26`'s shochet and zorek are built (with `o.p27`; shift/index.js passes `p27: !globalThis.__SH27_OFF`); `P26.open` = menorah, inner, menorah, **shochet, zorek** — morning 4 the shochet, morning 5 the zorek (each as early as the schedule allows; the order is a choice, the owner may swap them); from morning 6 the draw is among the four built roles, never yesterday's. His blood role holds the morning (`gate27` in `gateAny`: the clock, the fire's drain, the stations) until it is done; that morning he never goes into the Heichal (`gate26` does not hold for a blood role). **The court around him:** when his role is a blood one, the inner roles' winners open the great gate 9 s after his lot (`gateNpc27`; heichal26's "השער הגדול נפתח!") and tend the five lamps 8 s after the gate — the order "והטבת חמש נרות קודם לדם התמיד" (Yoma 33a) is kept: a zorek who reaches the north-east corner sooner holds there, swirling, until they are done; when he is the shochet, the court's zorek receives, walks round and gives both gifts and the rest (dam27's `npcZorek`); when he is the zorek, the court's shochet cuts once the gate is open and he is at the vessel; when his role is the menorah or the inner altar, the court's shochet and zorek are build 26's (heichal26.js, only switched off for the blood roles). (2) **The shochet:** to the rings north of the altar by the left stick on the strong path; "של שחר היה נשחט על קרן צפונית מערבית, על טבעת שניה" (Tamid 4:1); within reach he glides to his place east of the lamb, facing west ("השוחט, עומד במזרח ופניו למערב"); **the binding** — "לא היו כופתין את הטלה, אלא מעקדין אותו" (Tamid 4:1), "יד ורגל, כעקידת יצחק בן אברהם" (Tamid 31b): a right-thumb drag along the guide brings the foreleg to the hindleg (no rope or ribbon; two court kohanim hold it, "מי שזכו באברים, אוחזים בו"); let go short = a free retry (counted in the mastery); then "וכך היתה עקדתו, ראשו לדרום ופניו למערב"; **the wait** for the great gate ("לא היה שוחט השוחט, עד ששומע קול שער הגדול שנפתח", Tamid 3:7); **the cut** — a needle sweeps a 300° dial back and forth (1.4 s one way the first time, then 1.15 s), the gold zone is [0.425, 0.575]; a right tap inside = PERFECT ("בדיוק! ✦"), outside = "עוד פעם" and a free retry. PEGI-7: the knife is a silver bar with a gold flash, no wound, no close-up of the neck (the view stays ≥ 8 amot off it); blood appears only as the receiver's stylized stream (`BLOOD.wet`, tagged by `tagBlood`). (3) **The zorek** ("שחט השוחט, וקבל המקבל", Tamid 4:1): **(a) receive** — a right-thumb horizontal drag keeps the mizrak under the wandering stream; within 0.15 of it the vessel fills (2.5 s in all), a rising tone and a buzz; **(b) carry** — the left stick walks, the right thumb circles (the vessel circles in his hand); 3 s without a circle and the clot meter ("קרישה", המחשה) rises; full = the throw is refused until he swirls it down — "ממרס בו… כדי שלא יקרש" (Yoma 4:3, labelled as said of the Yom Kippur bull's blood); **(c) the north-east corner** ("בא לו לקרן מזרחית צפונית, ונותן מזרחה צפונה"): drag back (the height) and sideways (the aim), release; the dashed arc and the target zone show where it lands; below the red thread ("וחוט של סקרא חוגרו באמצע…", Middot 3:1; "באלכסון מחצי המזבח ולמטה", Rambam, Ma'aseh HaKorbanot 5:6) and within ±3° of the corner's edge = the Γ on both faces ("ודמה טעון שתי מתנות שהן ארבע", Zevachim 5:4; "מתנה אחת כמין גמא נותן", Zevachim 53b) and the big word "שתי מתנות שהן ארבע"; within 28° = one face (counted 0.6); over the thread = the red cue **"מעל החוט — לא כאן"** and a free retry (the word "פסול" appears nowhere — a test checks); too weak or past the corner = a word and a free retry; **(d) the south-west corner** — round the north and the west ("מערבית דרומית, ונותן מערבה דרומה"), the same throw; **(e) the rest** — a right swipe down on the southern yesod: "שירי הדם היה שופך על יסוד דרומית" (Tamid 4:1); a short, crooked or slow swipe = a word and a free retry. (4) **Views, sounds, buzzes:** authored views (`camWork27`, CAM24's way, in shift/index.js's chain): the ring from the north, ≈ 11 amot off and 7 up (he east of the lamb, the lamb west, the legs between them; the neck ≈ 9.9 amot off); at each corner a view beside him — 62–65° round from the corner's diagonal, 10 amot off, 6 up — the edge from the yesod to over the thread in the frame's middle, his head in the lower left third, both faces seen (found by a pinhole search: the first views, from behind him, had his head on the corner's lower half because he stands on its diagonal); the yesod from the south-west; none while he walks. Six synthesized sounds added to audio.js's kit, which Phase B composes since 26c (`blade27`, `fill27`, `clink27`, `splash27`, `pour27`, `bind27`; the 26c guard stays green — its count excludes the build-27 names, which build27's own test voices); every buzz has a sound and a visual twin (the fill's tone, the needle's flash, the Γ's bloom, the red cue). (5) **Mastery, coins, book:** `mastery23('shochet')` = 0.3·time + 0.5·[1, .7, .45, .25][misses] + 0.2·[1, .7, .4][rebinds]; `mastery23('zorek')` = 0.2·time + 0.25·fill accuracy + 0.4·(gifts' mean − 0.15·retries) + 0.15·(1 − the clot's peak); the time scales 3–12 s and 14–36 s of his own hands' time; coins 3 (≥ 95 and quick) / 2 / 1, points 120·q + 20 if quick; the roles book shows both with "שיא · אחרון"; the summary line. (6) **The way:** the rings' floor (`rings27`) is walkable — stages.js's `'lishka'` footprint (x −54 … −22, z −62 … −27) had made the place of slaughter solid to him; while his blood role is on, the Ulam's lowest steps by the altar's west face ("בין האולם ולמזבח", Middot 3:6) are walkable too (`WEST27` / `westStrip27` in world22.js; the twin's pass and flow31's planner, with planner functions of their own so guide27's cached grid is planned again): the zorek's NE → SW way 116.5 → 71.4 amot, the ramp's foot → the ring 110.7 → 85.9.
- **Numbers:** **733 tests (723 pass, 10 skipped, 0 fail)** — `test/build27.test.mjs` 18 tests (the schedule and the draw, the held morning, the rings' floor — also walked in from the lamb pen's door —, the binding's free retry, no cut before the gate, the needle's window, the stream and the fill, the clot, the five lamps first, the throw's map, the two gifts and the rest, the records and the book, the views, the sounds, the texts as checked on Sefaria, a Divine-Name scan of the new files by code points); build26's and build26c's tests updated for the new schedule and names (with comments); `qa-content-scan` 12/12; `dist/temple3d.html` **5,456,505 B** (+39,399 over 26c's 5,417,106; the budget was +50 KB; 834,951 B under the 6 MiB cap); out/index.html titled "משמרת 45 (בנייה 27) — בית המקדש השני". **The role from the dawn (autoplayer, final engine; quick / a child's pace):** the shochet 17.5 · 19.0 · 19.1 · 22.2 · 23.9 s (mastery 97–100, 3 coins) / 21.4 · 21.5 · 21.6 s (mastery 100, 3 coins) — the targets (≤ 40 / ≈ 50) met; the zorek 38.2 · 38.9 · 39.4 s (mastery 97–98, 3 coins; 36.0 s on a lamb morning with one deliberate throw over the thread, mastery 89) / **50.4 · 50.4 · 50.8 · 51.6 s on pyre mornings** (mastery 68–89, 1–2 coins — 68 when both gifts fell on one face: the child bot's aim spread against the ±3° edge) and **40.0 s on a lamb morning** (mastery 84) — the quick target met, the child's ≈ 50 met on a lamb morning and 0.4–1.6 s over on a pyre morning. **A child's zorek morning, phase by phase (pyre):** the pre-dawn task 35.5–38.7 s; down the ramp to the second lot 9.8–9.9; the lot 3.3–3.5; to the vessel 12.1–12.2; the court's cut 1.5; receive + carry to the north-east corner + the first gift 8.9–9.6; round to the south-west + the second gift 11.8–12.6; the rest 2.1–2.6; the whole morning 86.3–90.3 s (a lamb morning 73.9; quick 59.8–65.2). The shochet's whole morning 46.3–49.8 s quick, 57.8–60.9 a child's. His own hands' time: the shochet 1.4–3.8 s, the zorek 14.9–22.5 s. 0 stuck and 0 page errors in every run on the final engine (one earlier `--book` run reported the bot's own "stopped" as a page error — the runner now filters it).
- **Verified by:** node tests; 31 autoplayer runs and a probe through `brun` (the bot taught both roles: the binding drag along the guide, the tap in the gold, the stream followed, the swirl on the way, the throws, a deliberate throw over the thread with `--high27`, the swipe; phase shots with `--shots27`, the roles book with `--book`); PNGs, each opened (scratchpad `auto/<run>/shot-<phase>.png`): the binding (the lamb on its side, the two holders, the moving ring on the foreleg, the dashed target ring below it, "⬇ גררו: יד ורגל"), the cut (the dial with the gold zone and the needle, the lamb bound, "שחטו בזהב"), the court's zorek with the vessel at the neck while he is the shochet (`npcRecv`), the receiving (the stream, the lane gauge, the vessel 44 %), the carry (the clot meter, the gift boxes מז״צ / מע״ד, the stick zone), the north-east aim (the dashed arc to the corner below the thread), the throw over the thread and the red "מעל החוט — לא כאן", the Γ on both faces with "שתי מתנות שהן ארבע", the south-west aim and Γ, the pour on the southern yesod, the roles book (השחיטה 100 % · 1 פעם, זריקת הדם "עוד לא יצא לך", 9 roles "כהנים אחרים — בקרוב"). **Bugs found by the runs and fixed:** the binding never fired in the browser (`view.project` returns one shared object — both legs were the same point); the ring's view had him in front of the lamb (moved to the north, higher); the zorek's NE → SW way went round the ramp's foot (the west strip); on a lamb morning the second lot is at the pen's door, east of the rings, and a 0.4-amah strip between the rings' floor and the footprint's edge was an unseen wall (the child autoplayer stood at x −22.0 for 100 s — `rings27`'s east edge now runs past the footprint; a test walks the way); the binding's guide from the north view was ≈ 10 px long and pointed down while the arrow said ↔ (a sideways drag did nothing and said nothing — now the guide is 76 px along the legs' own screen direction, the arrow is the guide's, a wrong-way drag gets a word); the corners' views had his head on the corner.
- **What remains:** the child's zorek on a pyre morning is ≈ 1 s over ≈ 50 s (the 9.9 s down the ramp to the second lot is 26b's; the walk to the rings is 86 amot); the court's zorek at the north-east corner was never caught in a frame (`npcNE`: the drawers do not run while the autoplayer simulates — seen only in the drawer's code path and the node tests); the 0.8 s "kavanah" hold before the cut (`shchita-kabala-he.md`) is not built; the sounds and buzzes were never heard or felt (headless); the views were tuned on a 640 × 296 frame; the recovery's parity tool no longer applies to the modules build 27 changed (day33, focus17, heichal26, flow31, world22, index.js, audio.js).
- **Deviations:** (1) the shochet himself makes the binding gesture — the Mishnah has "מי שזכו באברים, אוחזים בו" (they are drawn holding it; the gesture is his because the role must have a hand in it); (2) the Ulam's lowest steps west of the altar are walkable while a blood role is on (a game route, so the receiver does not go round the ramp's foot); (3) the "ממרס… כדי שלא יקרש" line is Yoma 4:3's, said of the Yom Kippur bull's blood — shown with that label as the reason for swirling; (4) the court opens the gate 9 s after his lot and tends the five lamps 8 s after the gate (game timings, המחשה); (5) the ring's view comes from the north, not from behind or beside him as the spec drew it (from there he hid the lamb); (6) the lamb is drawn ×1.35 against build 26's court lamb (as the other works draw a lamb); (7) the 26c guard test's names count excludes names ending in 27 (build27's test voices them).

### build 28 — the cleanup: what in the shipped game contradicted the owner's decisions (22.10; docs/specs/rohvi-he.md "סדר בנייה מוצע", hachraot-he.md "המערכות הרוחביות (22.10 09:19)"; the new module `src/avodah/shift/prefs28.js`)
- **What:** eight items in the shipped flow (out/index.html → `__FORCE_SHIFT` → shift → lobby35 → day33); the service itself (the morning's acts, their scores, the gates) is untouched. (1) **No 24 h expiry:** `DAY33.saveMaxH` is gone; `savedDay33` checks only the save's shape (an object, not an array, `day ≥ 1`), so "המשך יום N" resumes however long the player was away — the day, the gates passed (`washed`, `p23day`, `d21day`, `p25day`, `p26day`, `r26day` …), the fire (fuel, fed → level), the lambs, the tasks; nothing is lost by staying away. (2) **No "משמרת", no "סיור":** the lobby's mode pill is gone (one mode, the day — `st.mode` is always 'day', `pick('mode:…')` refused, a save's `lobbyMode` 'shift' / 'tour' opens the day; the full lobby's "שיא המשמרת" row went with it); the day's pause card has no "לסיור"; shift/index.js gives the lobby no `shift` / `tour`; the shipped page (`__FORCE_SHIFT`) now opens the lobby for any link — `#shift=N` (the 90 s round) included, `#shift=day` the day (main.js `startGame`; the plain dist keeps every link for the tests and tools). The code stays (`engine.js`, the engine22 day, `SH.run`, `SH.tour`, hud.js's `tour` branches), reached by no door. (3) **The side doors to the old Phase B home:** (a) `body.sh28` (prefs28's `cover28`, set when the lobby, the day or the 90 s round comes up — `goLive` in shift/index.js — not at the mount, so the first-run name and mishmar screens keep their bar as they were) hides the old `.av-top` (⌂, the status, the mute) — it neither shows nor takes a tap; the mute stays reachable as a switch "🔊 צליל" on the lobby's start screen (and, icons only, on its full screen) and on the pause card — the one mute of 26c (`sfx.muted` → `bh_avodah_mute`), ≥ 44 px; (b) the 1 s tick in avodah/index.js: once the shift is up (`SH.live`) a turn to portrait calls `SH.turn28(port)` — never `D.exit()` or `ui.showHome` — and back on landscape re-enters if needed. In the lobby that shows fable29's "turn the phone" card (`turnCard`, moved to prefs28 and shared with the portrait start; over a day it adds the line "המשחק מחכה — השעון עצר"), the world stays entered; in the day, focus17's own card (build 17: "סובבו את הטלפון לרוחב 🔄" / "המשך בכל זאת", the day paused by it) already covers it, because focus17's `__SH17UP` keeps `D.canEnter()` true — so the side door was the lobby's (and any screen without focus17); `createTurn28` pauses a day that is not paused (and resumes only what it paused; a pause the player made stays his). (4) **The first lot — one card:** `draw23` from the fourth morning (`P23.draw`, was `P23.two`) draws ONE role, never yesterday's, the least played weighted first — as `draw26` does; mornings 1–3 unchanged (the ashes, the pyre, the lamb); pis23's card is always the one card with its record line ("השיא שלך: N%" / "תפקיד חדש!") and its source, "הִתְקִינוּ שֶׁלֹּא יְהוּ תוֹרְמִין אֶת הַמִּזְבֵּחַ אֶלָּא בְפַיִס" — יומא ב, ב · טיוטה (checked on Sefaria); no "במה תבחר?", no 3 s time bar; the step card's "במה תבחרו?" line is gone; the engine's choice phase stays for a lot of two, which no draw gives. (5) **"משחק קצר" only from the opening screen:** the day's 12 s idle offer is gone (`DAY33.idleOffer` removed; the way to the next station instead; the night's Moked offer — off in the shipped day, `B17.night` — is titled "משחק לילה"); the lobby's start screen has one door "🃏 משחק קצר" (bottom right) opening a small picker of the three interludes — זיכרון המקדש (tiles), הוצאת הטלה מהדיר (pen), משחק האוצר (match3) — each through the lobby's own `play(key)` (its runner, its פרוטות, `interludeScore`), the picker closing as one starts; the full lobby keeps its one "זיכרון המקדש" door. (6) **The nedava at a fixed moment:** `nedavaStep` pays once a day when the day's clock reaches `C18.nedavaAt` (140 s; dayStep calls it every tick) — `nedavaP` and the rng are gone (it was `rng() < 0.3` at a gold streak or a tamid on time, from 20 s — in practice the first such event). (7) **The buzz's off switch:** "📳 רטט" on the lobby's start screen and the pause card, kept in `bh_haptics` (default on); ONE guard — `installHaptics28`, installed once in `mountShift` — wraps `navigator.vibrate` on the navigator instance: every module of the shift and the interludes calls `navigator.vibrate(…)` looked up at the call (a test reads every call site in src/), so with the switch off the call returns false and nothing reaches the browser (switching off cancels a vibration under way with the browser's own `vibrate(0)`); switching on buzzes once (18 ms) with its sound and the button's redraw as twins. (8) **"משימות הממונה — יום N":** the full lobby showed `tasksForDay(1)` always; `tasksNow33(save)` gives the day the player goes on with and its own three tasks with their marks (a day saved at night → the next day's three, as the engine will set them: `tasksForDay(day, level by B17.levels, ['kiyor'])`). **Found by the browser checks and fixed:** on a 640-px-wide screen focus17's top bar (fire, step card, tamid; z 31 over the HUD's 30) lay over the pause button — the tap went to "תמיד של שחר בעוד…", and the pause card is now where the switches are mid-play: while the shift is up the bar is `width: max-content` up to 64 px clear of each corner and sits under the HUD's layer (z 29: the pause and choice cards dim it), a little smaller under 700 px, the step card's words on two lines rather than cut (a first try, 72 px with one line, cut them to "בדיקת …"; one with wrapping but no max-content width squeezed the bar into half the screen — both seen in the PNGs); the lobby's "☰ מסך הבית" / "⛶ מסך מלא" were 36 px high and its doors 42 px on a short screen — all 44 now.
- **Numbers:** **752 tests (742 pass, 10 skipped, 0 fail)** — `test/build28.test.mjs` 19 tests (the shape-only save; a day saved 48 h and 30 days ago resumes as one saved a minute ago — the day, the level, the fuel, the gates, the lot, the role; the lobby's "המשך יום 3"; no pill, a save's shift / tour → the day; the shift's doors and the shipped page's links (read as text: shift/index.js pulls the kohen's .glb, which only esbuild loads); the cover's CSS and `cover28`; the mute switch is the kit's; `createTurn28` on a real day engine — the card, the held clock, the resume, the player's own pause kept; the schedule 1–3; one card never yesterday's over 4 histories × 4 seeds; the card's markup and source; no "משחק קצר?" after 30 s idle; the picker runs tiles through the lobby (פרוטות 3 → 12, score 350); the nedava at 140 s, once, the same moment for three seeds, no rng in its code; the guard on a stand-in navigator — off: four patterns, none reach it; every vibrate call site in src/; the tasks of the day; a Divine-Name scan by code points of the changed files, spaced / hyphenated / pointed forms too); build16's, build18's, build23's, day33's and lobby35's tests that held the old behaviour updated, each with a comment; `qa-content-scan` 12/12; `dist/temple3d.html` **5,465,038 B** (+8,533 over 27's 5,456,505; 826,418 B under the 6 MiB cap); out/index.html titled "משמרת 45 (בנייה 28) — בית המקדש השני" (5,465,750 B). **Autoplayer (quick / a child's pace), 0 stuck and 0 page errors in every run:** morning 4 — the first lot drew one card, the ashes (yesterday the lamb), the second lot the shochet: the morning 46.1 s / 61.5 s, the pre-dawn task 29.6 / 37.5 s, the first lot 4.4 s from the circle to the card's end (was up to 3 s more with the choice); morning 6 — the first lot the lamb / the ashes (yesterday the pyre), the second lot the menorah / the inner altar (yesterday the zorek): the morning 68.0 / 70.5 s, mastery 98 / 93; a whole day 2 (quick): the nedava at 40.0 s before the summary (the day's clock at 140 of 180), the summary at 235.3 s; **the 48 h resume:** morning 4's save with its `at` moved back 48 h, `--continue`: "המשך יום 4", day 4 again with its lots held (no lot ran, `roles23.m` stayed 4), the nedava at 140.0 s, the summary at 180.0 s.
- **Verified by:** node tests; 6 autoplayer runs and 18 probe runs through `brun` (scratchpad `b28/probe.cjs`: A1 the lobby, the picker, an interlude and back, the switches; A2 the 48 h resume on the page, the buzz off while the laver and the lot play — 0 calls of `Navigator.prototype.vibrate` (counted by an init script under the page's guard), 37–40 stopped by the guard in each run — the pause card and the buzz on from it, calls reaching the browser again; A3 portrait in the lobby and mid-day, back to landscape; B the one-card first lot; C a first run (no save): the name screen with the old bar as it was, no `sh28`; D `#shift=3` and `#avodah=live` on the shipped page → the lobby; T the three interludes from the picker, each on screen and each left by its own button — match3 only by scrolling its map card, below; F the full lobby). PNGs, each opened (scratchpad `auto/b28probe/`): `A1-lobby-start.png` (the start screen: "המשך יום 3", "משחק קצר" — זיכרון · הדיר · האוצר, "🔊 צליל", "📳 רטט" at the bottom right; the old ⌂ and mute gone), `A4-lobby-switches-off.png` ("🔇 צליל כבוי", "📴 רטט כבוי", dashed), `A2-picker.png` (the picker: the three games, "המחשה", "סגור"), `A5-pause-card.png` (הפסקה: המשך · מהתחלה · 🔊 צליל · 📴 רטט כבוי · העתק לוג — no "לסיור"; the pause button clear of the top bar), `B1-lot-one-card.png` (morning 5: one card "הפיס: בדיקת הטלה · השיא שלך: 100%", the Yoma 2:2 line, "☝ זכיתם!" over his hand), `A6-portrait-lobby.png` (the turn card over the lobby — with "המשחק מחכה — השעון עצר", which the last change keeps for a day only), `A7-portrait-day.png` (focus17's "סובבו את הטלפון לרוחב 🔄 / המשך בכל זאת" over the paused day), `A8-landscape-back.png` (the day again), `T-tiles.png` (זיכרון המקדש's start card over the lobby — taken with reduced motion: SwiftShader draws a frame in seconds, and the games' 0.35 s CSS fade-in had left the first shot, `A3-tiles.png`, at opacity 0), `T-m3.png` (משחק האוצר's map), `F-lobby-full.png` (the full lobby: "משימות הממונה — יום 3" with two of its three tasks struck ✓, the switches as icons at the top left, no pill), `C-firstrun.png` (the first run's name and mishmar screen, unchanged, its old bar still there). The last change — the turn card's clock line only over a day — came after the last browser run (node-tested).
- **What remains:** the sounds and the buzzes were never heard or felt (headless; the guard counts calls, it cannot feel them); match3's map card on a 296-px-high screen has its "שחק" / "חזרה לבנייה" buttons below the fold (the card scrolls), and its words say "חזרה לבנייה" (Phase A's) when it opens from the Temple's lobby — build 14's, not changed here; the night's Moked offer ("משחק לילה") and the 90 s round's own pause and end cards still carry their old doors in code (both unreachable in the shipped page); the full lobby keeps only "זיכרון המקדש" as a door (pen and match3 through the start screen's picker); focus17's portrait card offers "המשך בכל זאת" (a forced landscape) while the lobby's turn card does not — two looks for one moment; the recovery's parity tool no longer applies to the modules build 28 changed (day33, pis23, lobby35, hud, shift/index.js, avodah/index.js, main.js).
- **Deviations:** (1) mid-day the "turn the phone" card is focus17's (build 17), not fable29's — it already pauses the day and resumes it, and it keeps its "continue anyway"; fable29's card (`sh-turn28`) shows where focus17 is not (the lobby); (2) the switches are also on the full lobby (icons only, top left) so the mute is reachable on every lobby screen; (3) the full lobby's "שיא המשמרת" (the 90 s round's record) was removed with the pill; (4) the night's offer was renamed "משחק לילה" (it was "משחק קצר?", now the start screen's door's name); (5) `#avodah…` on the shipped page never reached main.js anyway (the head script turns any other hash into `#shift`) — the probe confirms both links land in the lobby; (6) the lobby's 36-px "☰ מסך הבית" / "⛶ מסך מלא" and 42-px doors were raised to 44 px, and focus17's top bar re-laid out while the shift is up (both found by the probes; prefs28's CSS — focus17.js itself unchanged).

### build 29 — the opening screen at the real hour and the Shabbat and Yom Tov lock (22.10; HANDOFF rule 16; docs/specs/rohvi-he.md §1–§2, rohvi-inventory.md rows 1–4; hachraot-he.md "שבת וחג"; realtime-dual-timeline-he.md "החלטות הבעלים (22.10 08:59)"; the new modules `src/avodah/shift/time29.js` and `src/avodah/shift/lock29.js`)
- **What:** on calendar.js's existing engine (the NOAA sun, the Intl Hebrew calendar, templeSchedule — no new astronomy). **calendar.js** takes an optional time zone (`sunTimes`, `templeSchedule`, `nowInTemple`: Jerusalem by default — every old call unchanged) and gives day-number helpers (`civilDay`, `sunOnDay`, `hebrewOnDay`, `weekdayOnDay`, `scheduleOnDay`). **(A) Where the phone is** (`time29.js` `where29()` → {lat, lon, israel, source, city, tz}): no permission asked — the device's time zone (Intl) → a city of a table of 67 zones (Asia/Jerusalem, Tel_Aviv, Hebron → Jerusalem, Israel; the US and Canadian zones, London, Paris, Belgium → Antwerp, Amsterdam, Berlin, Zurich, Rome, Moscow, Kyiv, Johannesburg, Sydney, Melbourne, Buenos Aires, São Paulo, Mexico City and more); a valid zone not in the table → the sun of its UTC offset (15° of longitude an hour, 40° N or 33° S), abroad; no zone ('UTC', none, invalid) → Jerusalem. "📍 מיקום מדויק" on the lobby's start screen beside צליל / רטט (off by default, ≥ 44 px): `navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition` once, on the tap only; kept in `bh_loc29` as {on, lat, lon} rounded to 2 decimals (≈ 1 km) with the Israel flag; a second tap forgets it; nothing is sent anywhere. **(B) The lock** (`status29(now, where)`): from one hour before candle-lighting (18 minutes before sunset; 40 within 20 km of Jerusalem) to צאת הכוכבים (the sun 8.5° down, calendar.js's `DEPRESSION.tzeit`) at the end; consecutive days one lock (Rosh HaShana's two days; a Yom Tov beside Shabbat — Shavuot 5787 on Friday 11.6.2027 runs from Thursday's candle-lighting − 1 h to Saturday night, no gap). Yom Tov days by the Hebrew date of the local civil day: Israel — 1, 2, 10, 15, 22 Tishrei, 15 and 21 Nisan, 6 Sivan; abroad also 16 and 23 Tishrei, 16 and 22 Nisan, 7 Sivan ("שמחת תורה", "אחרון של פסח"); Chol HaMoed, Chanukah, Purim, Erev Pesach, 9 Av open. **The calm line** 15 minutes before the lock — "עוד מעט שבת — המקדש ייסגר ב-HH:MM" (חג / יום הכיפורים), one fixed line, no countdown, no buzz, no sound: on the lobby (start and full screens), over the day under its top bar (`#s29-soft`, placed under focus17's bar), on the pause card (hud.js `pauseCard({ note })`). **At the lock** (shift/index.js's watch, `createWatch29`, every 2 s once the shift is up, and the gate `gate29()` in `card()`, `runDay()` and avodah/index.js `start()` before the first-run name and mishmar screens): the day saved exactly as it is (day33's own `saveState('lock29')`, then `E.stop()` — build 28's no-expiry save, so "המשך יום N" goes on after), stopped, an interlude's screen taken off (`.tl`, `.pn`, `.m3`), and the lock screen; a page opened during the lock shows that screen alone; in portrait no "turn the phone" card over it (its own dark backdrop). **The lock screen** (lobby35 with `o.lock`, lock29.js `lockHTML29`, z 9100 over everything): the lobby's court at the real hour's light, at rest — no kohen, no lamb, no motes; its own view (`LOBBY29.lock`: the laver, kohanim standing, the altar's side with its red line — chosen from eight framings in the browser checks) and the card at the bottom left so the court shows above it (portrait: the card in the middle over a dark backdrop) —; "שבת שלום" / "חג שמח" / "שבת שלום וחג שמח" / "שנה טובה" / "גמר חתימה טובה", the day's name, "המקדש ייפתח במוצאי שבת / החג / יום הכיפורים, בשעה HH:MM" (the time only), "בירושלים כבר נכנסה השבת" (החג, יום הכיפורים) while Jerusalem's began earlier (its candle-lighting, 40 minutes before its sunset) and has not ended (on New York's Sukkot 2 Jerusalem is in Chol HaMoed — no line); the verse — Shabbat "וְשָׁמְרוּ בְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל אֶת הַשַּׁבָּת" (שמות לא, טז), Yom Tov "וְשָׂמַחְתָּ בְּחַגֶּךָ" (דברים טז, יד), Rosh HaShana "יוֹם תְּרוּעָה יִהְיֶה לָכֶם" (במדבר כט, א), Yom Kippur "כִּי בַיּוֹם הַזֶּה יְכַפֵּר עֲלֵיכֶם לְטַהֵר אֶתְכֶם" (ויקרא טז, ל, cut before the Name) — each טיוטה; the footer "המקדש נח — המשחק שומר שבת וחג · הזמנים לפי …" (abroad: "חוץ לארץ: גם יום טוב שני"); the switches צליל / רטט; no door to play. **(C) The opening screen at the real hour:** the court lit by the real sun where the phone is (`sky29`: schedule.js skyOf's thresholds — night · dawn = עלות השחר → an hour after sunrise · noon · dusk = the last 90 minutes before sunset → צאת הכוכבים — by the LOCAL civil date: skyOf takes Jerusalem's, which says "night" at a New York dusk) through main.js's presets dawn / noon / dusk / night (the readable moonlit night, HANDOFF §3 — not the day's dark `nightd`); it was always dawn. "עכשיו במקדש: …" under the title — calendar.js's service now by the local sun with its first source in Hebrew (`srcHe29`: "Mishnah Tamid 3:2" → "תמיד ג, ב", "Yoma 33a" → "יומא לג ע״א") and טיוטה; between the acts "בין העבודות · הבא ב-HH:MM" (המחשה); at night a second line "בלילה הכהנים שומרים בשלשה מקומות" (מידות א, א). **Two doors:** the gold one stays the player's own track — "נקודת המוצא שלי" over "המשך יום N" (or "משחק") — the rewarding one, as before; above it "🕍 מכאן — המקדש עכשיו" opens a card: the hour, "לפי {city}", now and the next three acts with their times by the local sun ("≈" where calendar.js marks the time an estimate), each with its source and טיוטה, and "🔒 בקרוב מכאן: לשכת הטלאים · לשכת העצים" (a visible lock, no countdown) beside סגור; no reward. The day itself is unchanged. **The test hook:** `globalThis.__NOW29` (ms, ISO or a function), `__TZ29`, `__LOC29`; on a local page only (file:, localhost) also `?now=ISO[&rate=n]&tz=Zone&lat=&lon=` — the published page never takes them; `__SHIFT.status29()`, `__SHIFT.tick29()` for the browser checks.
- **The times this code computes (Friday 23.10.2026):** Jerusalem — the lock 16:18, candle-lighting 17:18 (sunset 17:58 − 40), Shabbat out 18:33 (IDT); New York — the lock 16:45, candle-lighting 17:45, Shabbat out 18:43 (EDT); London 16:32 / 17:32 / 18:39 (BST).
- **Numbers:** **773 tests (763 pass, 10 skipped, 0 fail)** — `test/build29.test.mjs` 21 tests (the lock table: Friday 23.10.2026 in Jerusalem and New York with their minutes, Rosh HaShana's two days as one lock in both, Yom Kippur, Sukkot 1 / Chol HaMoed / Shemini Atzeret in Jerusalem and Sukkot 2 / Simchat Torah in New York, Pesach 5787 — 1 and 7 / 1, 2, Shabbat, 8 —, Shavuot one day in Israel and two abroad (5788), the Friday Yom Tov into Shabbat with no gap; the days that stay open; the time-zone table and its fallbacks; "מיקום מדויק" kept rounded, nothing sent, asked on the tap only; the hook (file:/localhost only); the calm line — one fixed line for 15 minutes, on the lobby, over the day, on the pause card, nothing pulses; the lock mid-day on the real day engine — saved, stopped, the lock screen, at its end the lobby and the day resumed; the gate before the first-run screens; the lock screen's words and no door; the sky by the local date (New York's dusk, where skyOf said night); "עכשיו במקדש" and the guards at night; the two doors and the card, no reward; calendar.js's old calls unchanged; a Divine-Name scan by code points of every changed file and of every verse and line the screens can show); `qa-content-scan` 12/12; `dist/temple3d.html` **5,491,047 B** (+26,009 over 28's 5,465,038; 800,409 B under the 6 MiB cap); out/index.html titled "משמרת 45 (בנייה 29) — בית המקדש השני" (5,491,759 B). **Autoplayer (a weekday, Thursday 22.10, the real clock — open), 0 stuck and 0 page errors in both:** morning 4 (quick): the morning 49.2 s, the second lot the shochet, mastery 100; morning 6 (a child's pace): the morning 57.3 s, mastery 100.
- **Verified by:** node tests; 2 autoplayer runs and 9 probe runs through `brun` (scratchpad `b29/probe.cjs`, the shipped page with the clock and the zone faked by the page's own hook), 54 checks, all OK (and two runs of framings): **T** (22) the opening screen at Jerusalem's dawn, noon, dusk and night and New York's noon and evening (the world's preset as the game set it, the two doors, the line, every button ≥ 44 px), the "מכאן" card and no reward; **C** (6) the line between the acts and the compact card in Jerusalem and New York; **K** (10) Friday 23.10 in Jerusalem — the calm line on the lobby, over the day, on the pause card; the lock by the watch's own timer mid-play (the day saved, day 3); Friday night (the world at night), צליל reachable; **R** (6) the page reopened during the lock — its screen alone; Saturday 18:40 — the lobby, "המשך יום 3", the day resumed (day 3, resumed); Chol HaMoed open; New York's Sukkot 2 locked; **G** (5) "מיקום מדויק" with a granted position (Tel Aviv → 32.09, 34.78, Israel) and off again; the lock in portrait; **F** (5) the lock screen in its chosen view — opened in the lock, Friday night, Yom Kippur, New York on Friday night with "בירושלים כבר נכנסה השבת"; **V**, **W** eight framings of the court at rest. (The first K run was cut by its waiting shell's one-hour limit after K0–K4 had passed; K was split into K and R.) PNGs, each opened (scratchpad `auto/b29probe/`): `T1-jlm-dawn`, `T2-jlm-noon`, `T3-jlm-dusk`, `T4-jlm-night` (the night lines), `T5-ny-noon`, `T6-ny-evening` (New York's dusk at 17:45), `T7-here-card` (the first card — its סגור below a 296-px screen, fixed), `C1-jlm-dusk-line`, `C2-here-jlm-dusk`, `C3-here-ny-noon` (the card on one screen, "לפי ניו יורק", the afternoon Tamid 14:55), `K1-soft-lobby`, `K2-soft-day` (the line under the day's top bar), `K3-soft-pause`, `K4-lock-midday`, `K5-lock-jlm-fri-night` (the first framing), `K6-lock-reopened` (the "drag up" hint over it and the default follow camera — the twin fixed), `K7-after-shabbat`, `K9-lock-ny-sukkot2`, `G1-loc-on`, `G3-lock-portrait`, `V1`–`V4`, `W1`–`W4` (framings), `F1-lock-opened-friday`, `F2-lock-jlm-fri-night`, `F3-lock-yom-kippur`, `F4-lock-ny-fri-night`; the mornings' `auto/b29-m4/shot-0.png`, `auto/b29-m6h/shot-0.png`.
- **What remains:** the precise location moves into the parent area in build 31; the second door's chambers (לשכת הטלאים, לשכת העצים) are locked placeholders until the hooks (build 32 on); notifications are not built (build 31; never on Shabbat or Yom Tov); a zone not in the table is an estimate by its offset (latitude 40° N / 33° S) — the precise location is the remedy; a polar summer without צאת הכוכבים opens 72 minutes after sunset (no table city reaches it); the lock follows the phone's clock — a phone set to the wrong time is locked by that time; the old Phase B's `isRestDay` (streaks, Phase A) still uses the Jerusalem calendar (unreachable from the shipped page); the night preset reads in SwiftShader's shots, not yet seen on the owner's phone; the lock screen's four verses were checked against the Masoretic text from knowledge (as the brief asked), not opened on Sefaria in this session — they stay טיוטה until the owner's check.
- **Deviations:** (1) Rosh HaShana greets "שנה טובה" (the brief named "שבת שלום" / "חג שמח" / "גמר חתימה טובה" only); a Yom Tov on Shabbat "שבת שלום וחג שמח"; (2) a valid time zone not in the table is not Jerusalem but the sun of its offset, abroad — Jerusalem's sun would open the game in Tokyo or Lima on Friday night; only no zone at all (or 'UTC') is Jerusalem; (3) the URL hook works only on a local page (file:, localhost), so the published page cannot be unlocked by a link; the globals work everywhere (as any devtools could); (4) "בירושלים כבר נכנסה השבת" is shown only while Jerusalem's Shabbat or Yom Tov is on (on Saturday afternoon in New York, or on its Sukkot 2, Jerusalem's has ended — the line would be false), and also in Israel outside Jerusalem's 20 km (Tel Aviv lights 22 minutes later); (5) the lock's view is not the lobby's (his spot hidden, the camera moved to the laver and the altar) and its card sits at the bottom left — the court above it; (6) the "מכאן" card's times are by the phone's sun (the brief), so in New York the afternoon Tamid shows at New York's 8½ seasonal hours; (7) a lock that comes while an interlude is open removes the interlude's screen — its round is lost, nothing of the player's (פרוטות, levels) is; (8) the watch acts only once the shift is up (the first-run name screens are not interrupted; the gate in `start()` catches a page opened during the lock).

### build 30 — one rating for every act, and one line on screen during an act (22.10; the owner, 22.10 09:19 — hachraot-he.md "המערכות הרוחביות" 3; rohvi-he.md §14; the new modules `src/avodah/shift/rate30.js` and `src/avodah/shift/end30.js`)
- **What:** in the shipped day (out/index.html → `__FORCE_SHIFT` → shift → lobby35 → day33 and its role modules); Phase A (src/game, stages) keeps its own score and titles. (1) **One rating** (`rate30.js`, pure): `quality30(id, record)` → q 0…1 from each act's own accuracy, never time — the laver: the thumbs lifted mid-wash [1, .75, .6, .45]; the ashes: coals spilled [1, .65, .35]; the pyre: (0.25·unfit woods [1, .5, 0] (the first morning's warned one not counted) + 0.3·air + 0.15·wrong sizes [1, .5, 0]) / 0.7; the lamb: 1 − 0.55·blemished approved − 0.2·whole rejected − 0.2·dirt taken for a blemish (an approved blemish is טעון תיקון, HANDOFF §5א-4); the inner altar: its accuracy / 100; the menorah: (0.5·pour + 0.2·refused swipes [0 → 1, ≤ 2 → 0.6, else 0.3]) / 0.7; the shochet: (0.5·misses [1, .7, .45, .25] + 0.2·rebinds [1, .7, .4]) / 0.7; the zorek: (0.25·fill + 0.4·(gifts' mean − 0.15·retries) + 0.15·(1 − clot)) / 0.8 — builds 22–27's weights with the time share taken out. `rate30(q)`: **מהודר q ≥ 0.85 · כשר 0.5 ≤ q < 0.85 · טעון תיקון q < 0.5** (`RATE30`, rate30.js:12). Every act is rated — the laver (at its unlock), the ashes, the pyre, the lamb, the menorah, the inner altar, the shochet, the zorek (day33 `rec23` / `end30`, day33.js:3032 / 3058); the free retries stay. At the act's end the step card shows the word for 2.4 s (`RATE30.wordS`; ✦ מהודר gold with a glow, ✓ כשר cream, ↻ טעון תיקון soft sand — no red anywhere) and under the top bar one source line (`end30.js`): the quote and its reference (the reference never cut: the quote takes the "…"), tappable for 8 s or until the next act — the tap opens the act's card with every source line the act used to show (each module registers them at load: `reg30`, rate30.js:138) and pauses the day; after a טעון תיקון a second line "אפשר לנסות שוב בספר התפקידים". The laver's rise waits 2.4 s and the second lot waits for the end's word (day33.js:2231, 2919). (2) **Mastery = accuracy:** `mastery23 = round(100·quality30)` (day33.js:336); the 20–50 % time share, `M22.fast`, `P23.dT`, `P25` / `P26` / `P27` `.tT` and `.fast`, `D21.coins` removed; the saves' old values are not rewritten. Each record keeps `r30` {h, k, t, best}. **The skill titles** (draft, by the role's counts): מתחיל — played; **רגיל — 2 times כשר or better; מיומן — 3 times מהודר; מומחה — 6 times מהודר** (a role comes up every 3–4 mornings: רגיל in a week or two, מומחה after months). An older play (in `n`, not in `r30`) counts as כשר when its best was ≥ 50 % (never as מהודר — its mastery had time in it); its best rating from the old best % (`role30`, rate30.js:90). The roles book (pis23 `rolesBook23`) shows per role the best rating, "מהודר ×N", the title and the times, the laver's own row ("קידוש ידיים ורגליים — כל בוקר"), "שימשת N בקרים" and the titles' legend — no %. (3) **The day's summary** (day33 `summary17`, focus17 `showSummary`): the day's ratings as chips (the act's icon, name and rating), its acts, the coins, the tasks, the badges, a praise line by the ratings and "📜 המקורות של היום" (every act's sources again, without a clock — `openDay`); no stars, no points. **The lobby and the HUD** (lobby35, hud.js, shift/index.js): no ★, no ◉ dinars, no builder's title (`SCORE30`: the day and the lobby no longer feed Phase A's score); the start screen "🏛️ בית המקדש השני · שימשת N בקרים · <icon> <the highest title>"; the full lobby "שימשת N בקרים · 🪙 P פרוטות · 📜 b/15" and a card "המשמר שלך בעזרה" with the two highest titles, opening the roles book; the pause card "שימשת N בקרים". `save.served30` counts the mornings served (once a day at the first rated act of the morning; an older save starts from `roles26.m` / `roles23.m`, set once at the day's start). **The coins** stay: 3 / 2 / 1 by the rating (`pay30`) for the laver, the pre-dawn role and the second lot's role. (4) **One line during an act:** while an act is in progress (`E.act30()`) the step card is its ONE line (`LINE30`, rate30.js:109: 2–4 words per act and phase — "שני אגודלים — החזיקו", "החליקו הילך והילך", "כשר ימינה · פסול שמאלה", "האירו ומצאו כתם", "גררו: יד ורגל", "שחטו בזהב", "זרקו מתחת לחוט" …); a word of feedback replaces it for 1.6 s (`E.say30`); every other card is off the world in an act — the modules' hint cards, bottom lines, centre banners, the thumbs' cue words (icons only), the pyre's wood name tag, the morning's tamid timer (the coins' till stays), the lot's record and source lines (the source moved to the end's line); the task stamps, level and badge pops wait until the act and its word are over (focus17 `later30`). Every source text stays in the code (registered for the end's card); no act loses its source or its המחשה. (5) **Layout:** the end's line steps to the screen's other side, narrower (≤ 46 vw), when the kohen's head (hero35's head bone) is under it; the act's line on one line at 640 × 296 and 844 × 390 (the tamid timer hidden in an act); the pause button and the switches clear; every button ≥ 44 px.
- **Numbers:** **789 tests (779 pass, 10 skipped, 0 fail)** — `test/build30.test.mjs` 16 tests (the thresholds; quality per act from records; no time or speed in mastery — the code scanned and a slow and a quick engine ashes act rated alike; the coins by the rating; the laver rated; a shochet morning end to end — the record, `r30`, the coins, `served30`, the summary's ratings; `LINE30` 2–4 words and `say30`; the modules' words off the world in an act; every act's source at its end; the titles, `role30`, `served30`; the roles book; the lobby and the HUD without ★; no red in the ratings' colours; the mornings' count; a Divine-Name scan by code points of the changed files, the tools and this file); build17's, build20's, build21's, build22's, build25's, build26's, build26b's and build28's tests that held the stars, the fast bonuses, the time scales, the spill-coins and the lot card's source updated, each with a comment; `qa-content-scan` 12/12; `dist/temple3d.html` **5,506,908 B** (+15,861 over 29's 5,491,047; 784,548 B under the 6 MiB cap); out/index.html titled "משמרת 45 (בנייה 30) — בית המקדש השני" (5,507,620 B). **Autoplayer, 0 stuck and 0 page errors in every run (18 runs):** quick, mornings 1–5 — every act מהודר (the pyre q 0.991 / 0.983, the zorek 0.978), the pre-dawn task 26.8–32.2 s, the morning 51.4–67.4 s; a child's pace (h1–h5) — morning 1: the laver כשר 0.75, the ashes מהודר, the menorah כשר 0.829 (the morning 94.0 s); morning 2 with a deliberately poor laver (`--bad30`, three lifts): the laver **טעון תיקון 0.45 (1 coin)**, the pyre מהודר, the inner altar מהודר 0.85 (81.1 s); morning 3: the laver כשר, the lamb מהודר, the menorah מהודר (88.4 s); morning 4: the laver מהודר, the pyre מהודר 0.974, the shochet מהודר (71.7 s); morning 5: the laver כשר, the lamb מהודר, the zorek מהודר 0.882 (75.4 s); the child's pre-dawn task 35.6–43.4 s (the laver's 2.4 s word and the second lot's wait for the end's word are in it).
- **Verified by:** node tests; 18 autoplayer runs and 2 probe runs (7/7 checks each, 640 × 296 and 844 × 390) through `brun`; the autoplayer's `--shots30` (an act's line, its end, the source's card, the summary) and `--bad30`; PNGs, each opened (scratchpad `auto/`): the act's one line — `b30-q1/shot-k20.png` (the laver), `b30-q1/shot-d21.png` and `b30-w1/shot-d21.png` (the ashes; 844 px), `b30-q1/shot-men.png` (the menorah's pour), `b30-q2/shot-inner.png`, `b30-q4/shot-shochet.png`, `b30-q5/shot-zorek.png` (the zorek carrying); the end with מהודר and its source line — `b30-q1c/shot-end-kiyor.png`, `b30-q1c/shot-end-deshen.png`, `b30-q1/shot-end-menorah.png`, `b30-q2/shot-end-m22.png`, `b30-q3c/shot-end-lamb.png` (the line stepped aside from his head), `b30-q5/shot-end-zorek.png`, `b30-w5/shot-end-zorek.png`; a טעון תיקון end — `b30-h2/shot-end-kiyor.png`; the source's card opened by a tap — `b30-q1/shot-src-kiyor.png`, `b30-q1/shot-src-deshen.png`, `b30-h2/shot-src-kiyor.png`, `b30-q3c/shot-src-lamb.png`, `b30-q5/shot-src-zorek.png`; the summary — `b30-sum/shot-sum.png`; the roles book — `b30-sum/shot-book.png`, `b30probe/L3-roles-book-640x296.png`; the lobby without ★ / dinars — `b30probe/L1-lobby-start-*.png`, `L2-lobby-full-*.png`; the pause card — `L5-pause-*.png`. **Found by the browser checks and fixed:** the end's line cut its reference on 640 px; it stood on the kohen's head where the end's view frames him large (the lamb's); the act's line wrapped to two lines beside the held tamid timer (and the first fix hid the coins' till too — it shares the class); a pop at the top over the end's line; "שימשת N בקרים" counted today's second lot twice from an older save (`b30-sum/shot-sum.png` was taken before that fix — day 4 shows 5).
- **What remains:** the roles book has no practice door yet (the טעון תיקון line invites to it); the journal ("המשנה המלאה — ביומן הכהן (בקרוב)") is a later build; before a following lot the end's line is tappable for ≈ 2.5–3 s only (the summary's "📜 המקורות של היום" has them all); the day loop's station acts (the wood, the kevesh, the song, the pen station) are not rated; the ramp run's best time is still in the summary; a few feedback and milestone words through `say30` run over 4 words; the inner altar cannot reach טעון תיקון (its accuracy floor is 65); the court's narration outside an act (heichal26, dam27) is unchanged; a peek24 slot-tap pop can briefly overlap the end's line; the sounds were never heard (no new sound — the existing names reused); the recovery's parity tool no longer applies to the modules build 30 changed.
- **Deviations:** (1) the rating's word sits in the step card (the top bar), not a centre banner (those sat over the kohen); (2) the laver's rise 0.35 → 2.4 s with the lot on, and the second lot waits for the end's word (a child's pre-dawn task up to 43.4 s); (3) the shift no longer writes Phase A's score (`SCORE30`); points are still computed in the records (without the fast bonus) but shown nowhere; (4) the pyre's wood name tag removed (the first morning's line teaches which go left); (5) older saves: the best rating from the old %, older plays count as כשר when ≥ 50 %; (6) the lot card's source moved to the end's line; (7) the laver is rated and has its own row in the roles book (it is not a lot role); (8) the quality weights are this build's (the old ones renormalized; the laver, the ashes and the lamb by their own measures); (9) the summary has a "📜 המקורות של היום" button. **Rule lapse reported:** a `node -e "require('…/tools/autoplay35-run.cjs')"` meant as a syntax check launched Chromium outside `brun` (it died when its output pipe closed); afterwards only `node --check`.

### build 31 — practice any role you have had, from the roles book (22.10; hachraot-he.md "עולם ומבנה", the owner 21.10 23:46: "יום מלא, רצוף, מברקאי ועד שיר הלויים" AND "ספר התפקידים — אימון בכל תפקיד בנוי, לשיא"; "פתיחת תוכן" — the lot is the key; build 30's טעון תיקון end "אפשר לנסות שוב בספר התפקידים"; the new module `src/avodah/shift/practice31.js`)
- **What:** (1) **The doors** (pis23.js `bookHTML23` :29, `rolesBook23` :49): from the lobby (the full lobby's titles card "המשמר שלך בעזרה" and ספר הזכרונות › תפקידים — shift/index.js `openRoles31` :220, `openBook`) every role he has had in a real morning has "▶ לתרגל" (44 px) — the laver's row too; a built role the lot never gave him stays locked, **"עוד לא יצא לך בפיס"**; a role not built, **"בקרוב"** (the first lot's חביתין / הקטורת / השיר now say it with their names — they said "?" and "עוד לא יצא לך"; the second lot's nine "כהנים אחרים — בקרוב"); the incense: nothing (not built). "Had" is `timesOf` (day33.js:312) = n − pr: a practice run never opens a role. **Mid-day the book is read-only:** the day's summary now has "🎭 ספר התפקידים" beside "📜 המקורות של היום" (focus17.js:506, :517) — no doors, one line "לתרגל תפקיד — מספר התפקידים שבמסך הבית (המחשה)". ### build 32 — "יומן הכהן": one journal for what was scattered (22.10; docs/specs/rohvi-he.md §11 "יומן אחד; כל פתיחה מוסיפה עמוד עם המשנה" and §4, the four keys; future-unlocks-he.md "מה לוקחים" 2 — "עמוד חדש ביומן הכהן עם המשנה"; hachraot-he.md "פתיחת תוכן" (the first key: the lot) and "המערכות הרוחביות" 6 — "המשנה המלאה ביומן הכהן"; the new modules `src/avodah/shift/journal32.js` (the data, pure) and `src/avodah/shift/book32.js` (the sheet and the unlock card))
- **What:** (1) **One journal** (book32.js `openJournal32` :150, `journalHTML32` :141; z 63 — over the summary, the practice's end card and end30's card): a sheet like the roles book, its tabs ≥ 44 px and a ✕ of 44 (journal32.js `TABS32` :41): **תפקידים** — the roles book as it was (pis23 `bookHTML23` with `o.bare` :42 — its rows, ratings, titles, build 31's "▶ לתרגל" from the lobby; read-only mid-day); **משניות** — a page for every source he has met at an act's end (end30's registry `SRC30[id].all`: each line with a quote — journal32 `parse32` :122: its lead, the quote, a gloss, the reference, an המחשה note; one page per quote and reference by its letters, so a line two acts share — the great gate's, "חמש נרות", "שחט השוחט" — is one page, listed under each act met), grouped by the act in the morning's order (the laver, the first lot, its three roles, the second lot, its four), "חדש" until opened (a gold mark on the tile, the tab's count, the lobby's doors), the acts not met not listed at all (no spoilers) and "📜 N מתוך M משניות" over them (M = 49 now); a page: the quote large, its reference with "טיוטה", the note, "נכנס ליומן ב‹act›, ביום N" (an older save's: "מהבקרים שלפני היומן"), "הקודם / הבא" and its place in its act ("2/3"); **הבקרים שלי** — one entry a morning, newest first: "יום N", the real date where the phone is (`realDate32`, Intl he-IL, the zone of time29's `where29`) and the Hebrew date (calendar.js `hebrewDateAt` at that moment by the sun where the phone is — after sunset the next day; the year without its thousands, "י״א בחשוון תשפ״ז"), the morning's acts with their ratings (gold / cream / soft — no red); 100 entries kept (`J32.logMax`); "ועוד N בקרים מלפני היומן" when served30 counts more; **אותות** — BADGES33 as ספר הזכרונות showed them (✅ שלכם / 📜 "+N פרוטות — נרשם בעזרה, נגבה בלילה בבית המוקד"). The hooks' places (הטלה שבדקת, העצים שהבאת, העורות, התפוח) and the incense's once-in-a-life page sit in `TABS32` with `on: false` and no page — nothing of them is drawn. (2) **The data** (journal32.js, `save.journal32` = { v, acts, seen, log, hello }): `jr32` :68 makes it once — at the shift's mount (index.js :67) and the day's start (day33.js :961) — from what an older save had: the roles had (n − pr > 0; a practice-only role not), the laver, the two lots held → their acts met, their pages "חדש"; no log entry is made up, and `hello` is true (it was not his first morning). day33.js (:3165–3222): at every act's end in a real morning (`end30` → `j32end`, :3123; never in practice) `meet32` records the act (its pages are his from then on — collected, never lost) and `log32` adds its rating to the day's entry (made at the morning's first rated act: the real time — time29's `clock29`, or `o.now` — and the Hebrew date); `morning30` marks the entry served (`served32`, :3162); the lots' pages at the lot's end (`j32lot` in p23done, :3054 / :3078; no card). (3) **The unlock moment** (the first key, the lot): the first time a role of the lot ends in a real morning — or the journal's first page ever — `st.j32q` is set; as the act's rating word ends (`J32.cardS` = `RATE30.wordS`, 2.4 s) the tick (`j32Step`, :5241, before the day's steps) pauses the day and emits "j32new"; book32's `createUnlock32` (:270, made by focus17 with the day's modules, :413) shows the card (z 60, `unlockHTML32` :259): "עמוד חדש ביומן: ‹icon› ‹the role›" (the first ever: "📖 היומן שלך נפתח" and "עמוד ראשון: …"), the role's main source line (its end line's page: the quote and "‹reference› · טיוטה"), "📜 N מתוך 49 משניות ביומן שלך", one line, **"📖 לפתוח ביומן"** (the journal on that page, read-only; the day waits until it closes) and **"המשך"** (52 px); no countdown, no timer, nothing lost by "המשך" (`E.j32done`); the sound 'lyre' (an existing one). Never in practice, never on a repeat (the act was met), never for the laver except as the first page ever; the second lot waits under it (the day is paused). (4) **The doors:** the full lobby's titles card and its ספר הזכרונות door are now ONE door, "📖 יומן הכהן" (lobby35 :232: the mornings, the two highest titles, "📜 משניות n/m", "🏅 אותות b/15", a gold "חדש N" — no pulse); the start screen has a small one, the icon over "יומן" with "חדש N", in a row with "🃏 משחק קצר" (lobby35 :206; compact so the row ends where the switches' row ends — a first, 112-px version with "📜 n/m" reached the kohen's legs in the PNG); `pick('journal')` → index.js `openJournal` (:226) — from either screen, so practice now opens from the start screen too and "חזרה" returns there with the journal on תפקידים; `openBook` → the journal's אותות (the old sheet only with `__SH_OLDBOOK`); `__SHIFT.roles()`, `__SHIFT.journal(tab, from, x)`.
