PNGs, each opened (scratchpad `auto/`): the unlock card — `b32-m12/shot-j32-kiyor.png` ("היומן שלך נפתח", "עמוד ראשון: 💧 קידוש ידיים ורגליים", "2 מתוך 49"), `b32-m12/shot-j32-deshen.png` ("עמוד חדש ביומן: 🔥 תרומת הדשן", "פנה את הגחלים הילך והילך…", "תמיד א, ד · טיוטה"), `b32-m34/shot-j32-shochet.png`; a mishnah page opened from the card — `b32-m12/shot-j32page.png`; the summary's sources — `b32-m34/shot-sum32.png` ("המקורות של היום — יום 3", "כל המשניות ‹"); the journal's four tabs — `b32probe/P-roles.png`, `P-mishna.png` ("49 מתוך 49", "48 חדשות", the tiles with "חדש"), `P-mornings.png` (five mornings, "יום חמישי, 22.10.2026 · י״ב בחשוון תשפ״ז" — the runs were after Jerusalem's sunset), `P-badges.png`; a page — `P-page.png` (its place then "1/59", the flat list — changed to the act's), `P2-page.png` ("1/2"); today's — `P-today.png`; the start screen with the journal's door — `P-start-door.png` (the first, wide door over the kohen's legs), `P2-start-door.png` (compact, clear of him), `W-start-door.png` (844 × 390); the full lobby with its one door and "בנייה 32" — `P-lobby-full.png`; 844 × 390 — `W-mishna.png`, `W-mornings.png`. **Found by the browser checks and fixed:** page.html's global `.x` (absolutely placed at the top left) took the journal's ✕ over the אותות tab (the ✕ is `.j32x`); the start screen's first door reached the kohen; a page said "1/59" (the flat list, shared lines counted twice) — now its place in its act.
- **What remains:** the card's sound ('lyre') and the journal were never heard or seen on a phone; a mishnah page shows the game's short quotes (each a draft with its reference) — not the whole mishnah text, which is not in the game; the illustrated pages of future-unlocks-he.md ("פירוש מאויר") are not made; the camera's crane and the light on the place at the unlock (future-unlocks-he.md "מה לוקחים" 2) are not built — the card only; the hooks' and the incense's pages are places in `TABS32` only; the mornings' dates are the phone's clock (a phone set wrong logs that date); a role's card waits for its act's end, so a morning abandoned mid-act shows it the next time the role ends; the practice pause card still says "חזרה לספר התפקידים" (it returns to the journal's תפקידים tab); the recovery's parity tool no longer applies to the modules build 32 changed.
- **Deviations:** (1) the first page ever comes at the laver (the morning's first rated act): morning 1 has three cards — the journal's opening, the ashes, the menorah; (2) an older save gets no "היומן שלך נפתח", and all its pages are "חדש" at once; (3) a page is a source line with a quote — the paraphrases with a reference ("הממונה סופר אצבעות · יומא ב, א", "הפיס השני: שלושה עשר כהנים — יומא ב, ג") and the המחשה notes stay in the act's end card only; (4) the lots are groups of the journal (their pages enter at the lot's end, without a card); (5) the titles' card is not removed but became the journal's door (its titles and mornings kept on it); the start screen's door is icon-and-name only; (6) the summary's "🎭 ספר התפקידים" opens the journal's תפקידים; end30's card footer changed; (7) the footer constant is new (the start screen had none — it said "בנייה 29" by hand); (8) practice opens from the start screen too (through the journal), "חזרה" back to the screen it came from; (9) the Hebrew year is written without its thousands; (10) the test rig answers the card at once — older tests that stepped past an act's end (build 30's shochet morning) needed it; (11) the runner's `--skip32`, `--open32 [id]`, `--src32` and the bot's card handling (it would otherwise wait under a paused day); the practice runs now open the book through the journal's door.

### build 33 — "הטלה המבוקר": the first real-time hook (22.10; docs/specs/time-gating-hooks-he.md hook 1, approved 22.10 08:53 — "בלי שם לטלה", "הפרס על המתנה = משהו שנראה בעולם ושייך לך — לא כוח"; hachraot-he.md "הוקים בזמן אמת"; realtime-dual-timeline-he.md — the "מכאן" door; the new modules `src/avodah/shift/lamb33.js` (the data, pure) and `src/avodah/shift/chamber33.js` (the cards, the morning's card, the journal's tab))
- **What:** (1) **The door:** the opening screen's "🕍 מכאן — המקדש עכשיו" card has **"🐑 לשכת הטלאים"** open (chamber33 `hereDoor33` :68 — "טלה אחד לבדוק היום" / "הטלה הבא — מחר", "N טלאים שלך בלשכה"; 239 × 48 px), **"🔒 בקרוב: לשכת העצים"** beside it (lock29 `SOON29` :18, `hereHTML29(tn, w, now, { door })` :79); lobby35 `door33` :205, `pick('ch33')` :267 → index.js `runChamber33` :288. (2) **The chamber** (Beit HaMoked's south-west chamber, "מערבית דרומית, היא היתה לשכת טלאי קרבן" — מידות א, ו): the day's engine as a practice-like run of the lamb on a COPY of his save (practiceSave31; the saved day untouched) with `o.practice.ch33` (day33 :772): he starts in the azara before the hall's door and walks in with the left thumb (CH33.start, lamb33 :55); the pen's own inspection (pen25, the right thumb) at the chamber's door in the hall — the pen's layout moved there (`CH33P` / `pl25` day33 :777; pen25 `PL` :58), so its authored views read it the same; **the day's ONE lamb at an easy level** — seeded by the real day number, the pen's first-lamb generation (big spots, one blemish in a plain place or a straw; `newLamb25`); the sky of the real hour where the phone is (sky29) and **by day no torch**: a light dusk round the look, the act's line "הביטו ומצאו כתם" (lamb33 `LINE33`; at night the torch as in the pen); the azara's station chips and the work button off, the fire neither drains nor warns (day33 :992, :4597, :5041, :5889). Outcome (`ch33end` day33 :3478): **תמים** → its tag and "נכנס ללשכה · מוכן בעוד 4 ימים", the lamb led through the chamber's door to its stall; **בעל מום** found → it does not enter, the blemish (המחשה) and the Rambam line the pen gives, "מחר יש טלה אחר"; approved with a blemish → shown ("יש בו מום — …", no delay), it does not enter, rated טעון תיקון; a whole lamb rejected → **a free retry** ("הטלה תמים — הביטו שוב", rate30's −0.2). Rated like any act (rate30) and paid by the rating (3 / 2 / 1) — nothing else: "ch33rec" at once → index.js :321 `inspect33` + the coins into his save; the end card as the rating's word ends ("ch33end"). **Already inspected today** → no lamb comes: the stock and "הטלה הבא — מחר". (3) **The stock** (`stock33` lamb33 :109, `stockHTML33` chamber33 :81): his lambs with their tags and their days to ready — "מוכן", "מוכן מחר", "בעוד N ימים", never hours; the court's lambs fill to six, "של הלשכה" (`L33.six`); his may be more ("ומוסיפין עד לעולם"); in 3D the stalls hold his (ear tags in their colours) and the court's, plain (pen25 `stockSt` :408 / `stock33` :412; avodah3d's six chamber lambs step out while the run is up, `chamberLambs`); Thursday and Friday where the phone is: **"יש די טלאים לשבת"** — "כְּדַי לַשַּׁבָּת וְלִשְׁנֵי יָמִים טוֹבִים שֶׁל רֹאשׁ הַשָּׁנָה" (ערכין ב, ה), a fact. (4) **The payoff** (day33 `tl33take` :3246, `tl33Step` :3268, the tick :5377): on a real morning (never practice, never the chamber's run) the oldest of his lambs inspected ≥ 4 days before is the morning's Tamid lamb: for the lamb's role it is the pen's first lamb — whole, tagged (`newLamb25`, `K.mine`) — and the card comes as it stands at the door ("הטלה שבדקת ביום שני עולה היום לתמיד", "טעון ביקור ארבעה ימים קודם שחיטה" — מנחות מט ע״ב, "בודקין אותו לאור האבוקות" — תמיד ג, ד); for any other role the card comes a moment after the first lot ("צאו והביאו טלה מלשכת הטלאים" — תמיד ג, ג); ONCE (`seen`), the day waiting under it until "המשך" (chamber33 `createTl33` :232, made by focus17 :425); the lamb leaves the stock (`offer33`, by 'me', its game day and real time), the morning's journal entry gets its chip; its tag where the lamb is drawn — the pen's lamb, the court's at the pen's door (`E.tl33tag`), the ring lamb of the blood roles (dam27 :528). A ready lamb waits three more days for his morning; on d + 7 the chamber's kohanim offer it (`settle33` :90, המחשה) — recorded "עלה לתמיד ב‹תאריך› — בידי כהני הלשכה", no guilt word. Nothing of it is power: no speed, no bonus. (5) **The journal** (journal32 `TABS32` :47 on; book32 `lambsTab` :143, chamber33 `lambsTab33` :304): **"🐑 הטלאים שבדקתי"** ("הטלאים" on a narrow phone) — each lamb: "נבדק: ‹יום, תאריך› · ‹התאריך העברי›", "מוכן: ‹יום, תאריך›", "עלה לתמיד: ‹…› — בבוקר שלך (יום N)" / "— בידי כהני הלשכה" / "בלשכה · ‹N ימים›"; the בעל מום ones counted; the sources (מנחות מט ע״ב; ערכין ב, ה ×3 lines; מידות א, ו), each טיוטה, and the game's choices as המחשה. (6) **Time:** the device's clock (time29's `clock29` — its test hook `__NOW29` / `?now=` on a local page, as build 29's) in the calendar where the phone is; **a day = the civil day there, local midnight to midnight** (`day33n` :66 — a child's "tomorrow", not the Hebrew day's sunset); it runs through Shabbat and Yom Tov (the lock locks the screen, not the count).
- **Numbers:** **829 tests (819 pass, 10 skipped, 0 fail)** — `test/build33.test.mjs` 16 tests (one a day and the midnight boundary; תמים enters with a tag, בעל מום not; ready after four days and the words; the oldest first; the court fills to six and his may be more; weeks away — offered by the court on d + 7, recorded; Thursday / Friday; the count through Shabbat with the lock on; the chamber's run on the engine — תמים, a found blemish, an approved blemish, a free retry, the end card's timing, the coins, his save untouched but through index.js; today's done — the stock; the morning — the oldest, whole, tagged, the card once, the day waiting, no coins from it, a resumed day without a second card; another role's card; never in practice nor in the chamber; the "מכאן" door and the lobby's wiring; the cards' markup; the journal's tab; days never hours; a Divine-Name scan by code points of the changed files and of everything the hook shows); build29's (the open door — SOON29 is the wood chamber alone) and build32's (the fifth tab, GAME_BUILD 33) tests updated, each with a comment; `qa-content-scan` 12/12; `dist/temple3d.html` **5,580,818 B** (+31,317 over 32's 5,549,501; 710,638 B under the 6 MiB cap); out/index.html titled "משמרת 45 (בנייה 33) — בית המקדש השני" (5,581,530 B).
- **Verified by:** node tests; **16 probe runs through `brun`** (scratchpad `b33/probe.cjs`, the shipped page, a returning player's day 3, the clock faked by the page's own hook — Monday 26.10.2026 → Friday 30.10 and three weeks on, Jerusalem; each mode's storage the next one's): the last set — A, B, C, D, E, T, then C and D again after the last card fix — 40 checks (+ 9 again), all OK, 0 page errors (the eight before it found the bugs below): **A** Monday — the open door (14), the walk in, the inspection by day, the straw brushed, תמים, his save (one lamb, tag 0, +3 פרוטות), the door "הטלה הבא — מחר", the chamber again — done; **B** Tuesday — a crack on the back found, "בעל מום — לא נכנס ללשכה", Monday's "בעוד 3 ימים", one blemished inspection recorded; **C** Wednesday ("בעוד 2 ימים", "בעוד 4 ימים", four of the chamber's) and Thursday ("מוכן מחר", "יש די טלאים לשבת"); **D** Friday (Monday's "מוכן", the line); **E** Friday's morning — the lot gives the lamb: the card at the pen, the day waiting, his lamb whole with its tag; offered by him on game day 3, seen once; the journal's tab (inspected, ready, offered, "בלשכה", the sources; five tabs 101 × 44 px); three weeks later "בידי כהני הלשכה"; **T** the stalls with four tags. **Autoplayer, 0 stuck, 0 page errors** (the runner's new `--lambs33 K`: his lambs K days ago by the device's clock): `b33-m3h` morning 3 at a child's pace — the card at the pen ("הטלה שבדקת ביום ראשון עולה היום לתמיד"), the laver מהודר, the lamb מהודר, the menorah כשר 0.686, the morning 96.9 s, then the journal's own card for the lamb; `b33-m4` / `b33-m4b` morning 4 — the card after the first lot ("… ביום שישי …", the older of two), the pyre מהודר 0.983, the shochet מהודר, the morning 50.2 / 49.8 s, the newer lamb left in the chamber. PNGs, each opened (scratchpad `auto/b33probe/`): `A1-here-door` (the "מכאן" card, the open door), `A2-walk-in` (the azara before Beit HaMoked, the chamber's stalls through the cut-away wall), `A3-inspect-day` (the lamb at the chamber's door by day, the straw under the focus ring, "הביטו ומצאו כתם"), `A4-tamim-stock` (the end card: ✦ מהודר, "תמים — נכנס ללשכה · מוכן בעוד 4 ימים", the blue tag, the source, the stock), `A5-here-tomorrow` (the door: "הטלה הבא — מחר · טלה אחד שלך בלשכה"), `A6-done-tomorrow` ("את הטלה של היום כבר בדקת · הטלה הבא — מחר"), `B0-blem-zoom` (the crack), `B1-blem-card` ("בעל מום — לא נכנס ללשכה", סדק · גב, the Rambam line, "מחר יש טלה אחר"), `B2-stock-tags`, `C1-wed-stock`, `C2-thu-shabbat` ("יש די טלאים לשבת" with ערכין ב, ה), `D0-fri-chamber`, `D0b-stock-tags`, `T2-stock-tags` (blue, gold, green, purple ear tags in the stalls), `D1-morning-card` (the morning's card at the pen), `D2-pen-tag` (his lamb at the pen by torchlight, its blue ear tag), `D3-lead-tag` (the seal "תמים לקרבן", the tag, led out), `D4-journal-tab`, `D4b-journal-offered` ("עלה לתמיד: יום שישי, 30.10.2026 · י״ט בחשוון תשפ״ז — בבוקר שלך (יום 3)", the sources), `D5-journal-weeks` ("בידי כהני הלשכה"); the autoplayer's `auto/b33-m3h/shot-tl33.png`, `shot-0.png`, `auto/b33-m4/shot-tl33.png`, `auto/b33-m4b/shot-shochet.png`. **Found by the browser checks and fixed:** the azara pen's chip ("דיר הטלאים 143") and the day's work button in the chamber; the day's fire warning ("🔥 האש דועכת!", with its alarm) over the stock; the stock's tags not passed into the run; the neck band hidden in the wool (ear tags now); the ring lamb's tag standing in the air while the lamb lay bound; the end card's buttons below the fold on Thursday (sticky now) and the Shabbat line under them (it comes first now); the tab's name cut on 640 px (a short word); the card's fade stuck in the probe's slow frames (no fade).
- **What remains:** the chamber is reached from the "מכאן" card only (the full lobby has no door to it); the walk in is a few steps (the inspection, not the walk, is the act); the chamber's cards and the morning's card were never seen on a phone, the sounds (bell25, lyre — existing) never heard; notifications (off by default, the owner's 08:59) are not built; the multiplayer's shared chamber (time-gating-hooks-he.md "משחק מרובה") is not built; the second hook (העצים) is next; the morning's lamb in a resumed day keeps the lamb assigned on that game day even across real days; the tags are drawn only in the chamber's run and the morning's scenes (the world's own chamber lambs stay untagged); a phone set to a wrong date counts by that date.
- **Deviations:** (1) **a ready lamb waits for his morning three days (d + 4 … d + 7); on d + 7 the chamber's kohanim offer it** — the brief's "on their day" made concrete (המחשה, stated in the journal); (2) day 3's word is "מוכן מחר" (the spec's "מחר"), days 1–2 "בעוד N ימים"; (3) the morning's card comes for the lamb's role at the pen as his lamb stands at the door, for any other role a moment after the first lot — and the day waits under it (as under the journal's card); (4) a whole lamb rejected in the chamber is a free retry, an approved blemish ends it as בעל מום rated טעון תיקון (the pen's delay and replacement lamb are the morning's); (5) the chamber's inspection pays coins by the rating but writes nothing in the roles book's record (the run's copy) — it is not the lot's role; (6) the morning's journal entry gets a chip ("הטלה שבדקת עלה לתמיד"); (7) the inspection stands in the hall before the chamber's door (the chamber itself holds the stalls), and the stock is seen from the south through the hall's cut-away wall; (8) the tags are ear tags (a band at the neck sank into the wool); (9) the tab's name on a narrow phone is "הטלאים"; (10) the probe injects `.sh-intro{transition:none}` for its slow frames (the page unchanged).

### build 34 — the naive kid's bug sweep: the dumb bugs a real child hits (22.10; the owner, 22.10 22:11: "תריץ שחקן אנושי שיעבור על (קודם כל) באגים דביליים"; no new feature — the fire loop's removal is the next build, svivat-avoda-he.md §3, untouched here; the new tool `tools/kid34/`)
- **What:** (1) **The naive kid** (`tools/kid34/kid.cjs`, `kidmodes.cjs`, `kidpage.js`; through `brun` only): a child of 8–12 who does NOT know the answers, real touch (CDP `Input.dispatchTouchEvent`, two thumbs), seeded — he reads the one line and tries its verb roughly (holds, swipes of every length and speed, drags, taps on any moment), pushes the stick toward the gold path he sees on the screen (and into walls), pokes every visible button (tap, double, triple, held), opens the pause card mid-act, turns the phone, reloads mid-act, idles 2 minutes, plays a first run (no save), a returning save and an older build-33 save, mornings one after another; the lobby's every door (start and full, "מכאן" and the chamber, "משחק קצר" and each interlude's first screen and back, the journal's five tabs and its pages, practice doors, the switches), the lamb chamber, the Shabbat lock with a faked clock. Its checks each look: page and console errors; targets < 44 px (an ::after hit area counted) or off-screen; buttons seen but covered; clipped or cut text; English or code on the screen; "?" placeholders; stale build labels; RTL glitches; overlapping text seen through; cards over the hero; unknown full-screen layers; buzz and sound calls reaching the browser with the switch off (counted under the page's own guards); coins changed by a tap; the engine's outcome events (ratings, the lamb's verdicts, swipe reasons); **stuck** — 60 s with no change of the screens, the act's phases, the gates or the line while he plays (soft), and **hard** when the scripted bot, called in after 75 s as a parent's hand, cannot move it on either. Diagnosis modes: `diag` (walk, games), `screens`, `sumdiag`, `firstrun`, `ch33cam`, `views`, `botlook`, `penwalk`.
  (2) **Fixed** (test/build34.test.mjs, 18 tests): **the journal after an interlude** — tiles/ui.js's global `.tl` and pen/ui.js's `.pn` (their sheets stay in the page after a game; a fixed full-screen layer at z 200 with a beige background) took the roles tab's legend `small.tl`, the lambs tab's long word `span.tl` and a page's nav `div.pn`: after "זיכרון המקדש" the journal was a blank beige screen and its ✕ took no tap (the child stuck until a reload) — now `.tl23` (pis23.js:49), `.tl32`, `.pn32` (book32.js:116, :161); **the journal's compact header on every phone** (book32.js:70 — the sheet is never wider than 660 px; at 740–932 px "🌅 הבקרים…" / "🐑 הטלאי…" were cut); the roles book's **laver row a centred block** (pis23.js:504 — inline-block, it split "יומא ב, א–ד · טיוטה" round it); **the day's pause card has "חזרה למסך הפתיחה"** (index.js:196 — the day had no way back to the opening screen but a reload; teardown → `E.stop()` saves the day, "המשך יום N"); **"העתק לוג" 44 px** (hud.js:320, an inline 32); **the turn card says "משחקים לרוחב"** (prefs28.js:120 — not the gone mode's "המשמרת"); **GAME_BUILD 34**; **the pyre's sort says why a swipe did not count** (maaracha22.js:703 — "ארוך יותר ↔" / "מהר יותר ↔" / "לרוחב ↔", as the ashes', the lamb's and the Heichal's do; it said "החליקו ימינה או שמאלה ↔" always and the kid sorted 0/4 for three minutes); **the first-run card fits a 296-px phone** (ui.css.js:110, :115, ui.js:313 — max-height 100vh − 60 with its 34 px of padding and border, the go row sticky: "יאללה לעבודה" lay below the screen and a thumb scrolled the mishmar list, so a first-time child could not start); **the summary's "המשך ליום הבא" sticky** (focus17.js:99 — at 640×296 it lay at 270–332 px; a tap on its sliver hit the backdrop); **the far stations' 34-px chips take a tap over 46 px** (focus17.js:109, ::after); **the chamber's walk-in view** (lamb33.js:63 `CH33.walk`, day33.js:3573 — the follow camera stood 4 m behind him in the strip before the hall, his head over the whole screen; build 33's own A2-walk-in.png shows it); **"מיקום מדויק" its 📍 alone ≤ 700 px** (lock29.js:28 — its words reached the kohen's feet at 640×296; "מאתר…" still shows); **the pen's start card on a 296-px phone** (pen/ui.js:113 — its title was cut; the small lamb picture goes there); **his Saturday is "מוצאי שבת"** (lamb33.js:164 `hisDayHe33`, chamber33.js:84, :303 — the screen is locked until Shabbat goes out; the morning's card said "הטלה שבדקת בשבת עולה היום לתמיד"; the court's offering on Shabbat stays "שבת"); **a resumed day no longer holds the zorek at "מרסו — חמש נרות קודם" for minutes** (day33.js:965 — the saved `men5t` / `g26t` were the old clock's and a new engine's clock starts at 0: after a reload, "מהתחלה" or the next visit following the gate's opening the first gift waited until the new clock reached the old — 129 s and more for the kid and the scripted bot; now they are past); **the step card's words take two lines up to 800 px** (prefs28.js:73 — at 740×360 the walking line beside the tamid timer was "בדיקת הטלה — לכו לדי…").

### build 35 — E1 of the work environment: the fire to the background, the morning measured by the work (23.10; docs/specs/svivat-avoda-he.md §1–§3 and §9 item 1 — the owner, 22.10 22:02: "האש לא במרכז. כל העבודה חשובה."; 22.10 22:58: "האיפיונים של הגימיני שסיכמנו עליהם טובים לנו. תשאיר אותם."; the engine's flag `o.e35`, its numbers in `E35`)
- **What:** (1) **Out of the morning** (day33.js `e35` — shift/index.js flags33 passes it on unless `__SH35_OFF`; the old loop's code stays behind it, with no door): the fuel's drain, the 180-s day and 60-s night, the tamid timers and their "late" (endDay17 has no tamid rows), the "נפתח:" stamps, the merchant, the lost lamb, the family's gift at 140 s (it rode the day clock), the old loop's badges (BADGE35: only the days' are earned); focus17.js: no fire ring (`.f17-top.f35 .f17-fire`), no tamid line, no warn/crit class — so no "🔥 האש דועכת!" banner and no alarm; the intro is "הכיור, הפיס — וכל העבודה חשובה" (index.js), the lobby's play button "הכיור, הפיס ועבודת הבוקר" / "הבוקר מחכה" (lobby35.js), the day's stamp "בוקר בעזרה — קודם לכיור"; the old loop's stations have no door (`door35`: runAt / tapStation false for the wood, the ramp's trips, the stores, the bakers, the old pen, the song, the pyre's laying) and no chip (`E.shown33` is empty — the salt chamber's chip carried the old loop's "קחו מלח — בזמן" and sat under the twin's pad at 844×390; the chamber's door and its bag stay, the work button at its door); the bar shows the laver's slot only while it is next (`bar17`) — the chain bar is E3's. (2) **The fire always burns, its look tells the morning** (`look35`, `fire35Step`, `lit35`): the night's fire before the morning's lot (0.85), the coals from the lot until today's pyre is lit (0.31 — as trumat-hadeshen-he.md and maaracha-he.md have it), then the pyre's look for the rest of the morning by the maaracha's rating — his (m22done), or the court's (d21done on an ashes morning, p25done on a lamb morning: כשר): מהודר 1.45 with sparks and a clean, thinner smoke (life.js `sparks35`, `__FIRE35`), כשר (and טעון תיקון, fixed by the elder kohen) 1.0; never drained, never a meter; an older save's lit pyre is read from its day (its rating, or כשר). (3) **The ramp run inside the maaracha role** (`run35` in ramp19Step): his sorted logs go up the ramp once — R19's pace on the ramp (the stick's depth the speed), the sway and the right thumb's hold (the work button "ייצבו את הסל — החזיקו"), a log dropped on bare stone when fast and unsteady (never on his first maaracha; the way and the line "הרימו את הגזר" lead to it; the stacking waits for it), the ghost of his best run and its time (its record at the ramp's foot only during the carry, ramp19.js); its coins are the act's; the run kept with the act's record (`m22r.run`). The salt chamber and its bag still salt the ramp. When the court has the maaracha its kohen carries the wood (pis23.js, as before). SWING17 stays (no door in the morning). (4) **The morning ends on its work** (`workDone35`, `close35Step`, `camWork35`): his acts and the court's done, his last rating's word over, no card up → the close: 0.6 s to dark with "הבוקר הושלם" (the scene changes there), the altar from the south-east (its fire as the morning's pyre burns, the court east of it), a calm card with "אש תמיד תוקד על המזבח לא תכבה — ויקרא ו, ו · טיוטה" (focus17 `close35`; the top bar, the bar, the chips, the acts' court lines and the twin's pad step aside, the stick rests), 4.6 s, then the summary under the morning's sky: "🌅 הבוקר הושלם · יום N · שימשת M בקרים", his acts with their ratings, "כהני העזרה: …" (the roles the lots gave the other kohanim, plainly), the ממונה's three by name (✓ / ○), the coins; no tamid rows, no logs, no ramp time. An older save whose morning was done goes to its close at once; E.debug.night() under e35 is the summary at once (the tools). (5) **The ממונה's tasks about the work** (`TASKS35`, `tasks35ForDay`, `done35`): "💧 מהודר בכיור", "🎭 תרגלת תפקיד בספר", "⭐ מהודר בתפקיד שלך", "🐑 בדקת טלה בלשכה", "✨ שתי עבודות מהודרות", "🆕 תפקיד שלא היה לך עד היום" — three a day, two places a day (never both of the opening screen's two), a task that cannot be offered gives its place (no role to practise on day 1; no role left he never had); each pays DAY33.taskReward 5 as before; the practice's and the chamber's are marked from the opening screen (index.js: `save.prac35`, `save.ch35` — the game day they belong to); the lobby's card shows them (`tasksNow33`). (6) **The pads:** "גרזן טוב" and "טנא גדול" hidden (`pads18`; fx18 draws the live pads); the laver's second spigot as it is (svivat-avoda-he.md §11.1, the owner's open decision). (7) **Older saves:** the old loop's basket, dropped logs, tamid states, lateness and clock are let go; the saved fuel is not the fire; the old tasks give way to the day's three of the work; "המשך יום N" as before.
- **Numbers:** **866 tests (856 pass, 10 skipped, 0 fail)** — `test/build35.test.mjs` 16 tests (the shipped flags and a whole morning plus 5 idle minutes with no drain, clock, tamid, "late", level or feeding; the HUD without the fire ring, the tamid line, the alarm or the banner, the intro and the lobby; no door and no chip for the old loop's stations, the salt chamber's door kept, the bar's laver slot; the fire always present — night, coals, pyre; מהודר high and clean, כשר normal, the court's pyre כשר; the ramp run inside the maaracha — the ghost, its time, the stick's pace, the hold; a dropped log and the stacking that waits for it, never on the first maaracha; the morning's end on its work — the fade, the view, the summary, never a clock; a shochet morning and the close that waits for a journal card; the summary's rows; TASKS35's pool, its three by the day, what gives its place; the tasks done and paid once, the opening screen's two; the pads; older saves — mid-day with fuel, clock and tamids, a done morning, a night; practice and the chamber unaffected; the Name scan by code points of every changed file); the old loop's tests in build26/28/29/31 run on `FLAGS34` (e35 off, the code behind the flag) or assert the new morning, build32/33/34's GAME_BUILD and lobby checks updated — each with a comment; `test/rig33.mjs` FLAGS carry e35; `qa-content-scan` 12/12; `dist/temple3d.html` **5,593,212 B** (+10,624 over 34's 5,582,588; 698,244 B under the 6 MiB cap); out/index.html titled "משמרת 45 (בנייה 35) — בית המקדש השני" (5,593,924 B). The mornings (the scripted player, engine seconds from the day's start to the summary; time shares sampled every 100 ms): a child (`--human`) on day 1 (ashes + menorah, the court's pyre) **103.1 s** — acts 58 %, the lots 7 %, the calm cards 7 %, the close 5 %, walking and waiting 23 %; day 2 (maaracha + inner altar) **87.5 s** — 52 / 9 / 8 / 5 / 26 %, the ramp run 4.5 s gold, 0 slips; day 3 (lamb + menorah) **98.1 s** — 47 % acts, 33 % walking and waiting; day 4 (ashes + shochet) **68.9 s** — 39 %, 33 %; the quick player on day 2 **65.1 s** (47 % acts, 24 % walking), days 5–6 (lamb + zorek **67.2 s**, maaracha + inner **62.5 s**; 47 / 45 % acts, 24 / 24 % walking; the run 2.9 s gold). The close is 4.6 s of every morning; the old 180-s day is gone.

The fire's look (scratchpad/b35/look2: the close's view at noon, מהודר against כשר — the high clean flame and its sparks against the normal one; scratchpad/b35/look: the dark dawn). The opening screen with an older mid-day save (its fuel, clock and tamids; the old tasks gave way to the day's three of the work) and with a save of this build (one task marked) at 640×296 and 844×390 (scratchpad/b35/lobby, final bundle; 0 page errors). **The naive kid on the final bundle** (tools/kid34): day K35-640-51g (640×296, the day-3 save; 4 findings — the act's source chip cut with its ellipsis (build 30's, by design; its tap opens the source), the step card's entrance caught at its first frame ×3; 1 soft stuck on the walk to the pen, the bot's hand moved it on; **0 hard, 0 page errors, 0 fire-loop findings**) and K35-844-52g (844×390, the day-5 save; 3 findings — the lot's 0.75-s flash `.p23-fl` (pointer-events none) not on the harness's list of known layers, the step card's entrance ×2; 1 soft stuck 17 s on the walk to the pen; **0 hard, 0 page errors, 0 fire-loop findings**). Before the last fixes: K640 (the old loop's badge "🔥 המערכה ברמה 2" popped — BADGE35; a hard stuck on the walk to the Heichal's gate, not met again), K640f (0 hard), K844f (the salt chip under the twin's pad ×17 — no chip now; 0 hard). The PNGs opened: b35-m22cg/shot-hud.png, shot-run.png ("ייצבו את הסל — החזיקו" on the ramp), shot-close.png (the high flame), shot-sum.png; b35-d1cf/shot-close.png (the court's כשר pyre), shot-sum.png; look2/look-close-mehudar.png, look-close-kasher.png; lobby/old-d3-full.png, new-d4-full.png (the tasks' card); b34-K35-640-51g/shot-04-f-clipped.png, b34-K35-844-52g/shot-05-f-fullscreen.png.

### build 36 — E2 of the work environment: four mastery levels for every built role, real Hard Fun without failure (23.10; docs/specs/svivat-avoda-he.md §1.4, §4.1–§4.5 and §9 item 2; the corrected table of gemini-koshi-kesharim-2-he.md "טבלת רמות מתוקנת"; the owner's "גו" to the plan — the open items' defaults: a level opens after two מהודר, the player chooses his level, no level goes down; the new module `src/avodah/shift/lvl36.js`, the tool `tools/lvl36sim.mjs`)
- **What:** (1) **The levels** (`lvl36.js`, pure: `LVL36[id][L−1]` = { he, add, src (טיוטה) or mh (המחשה), p (the act's knobs) }, `par36`; cumulative — a level keeps the conditions below it; the difficulty is content, never the day's pace or a penalty; build 30's rating unchanged, מהודר ≥ 0.85): **the laver** — L1 the ring fills (guided), L2 no fill (hold till the water stops), L3 the water runs ×1.6, L4 the stream tilts between the hands and the thumbs' heights level it (kiyor20.js: each ring's drag → `E.k20bal`; the tilt bar `.k20-bal`; a spill counts as a lift — "ימין על ימין, שמאל על שמאל", זבחים יט ע״ב); **the ashes** — L1 the pan lighter than before (wind/shake ×0.65), L2 each scoop toward the side where the consumed coals glow (`K.side`; deshen21.js `.d21-in36`; a scoop from the outer side takes none and is counted — "חתה מן המאוכלות הפנימיות", תמיד א, ד), L3 the full silver pan (×1.4, spill at 0.62 — "נטל את מחתת הכסף"), L4 heavier (×1.6, 0.55) and the heap on its exact mark (0.75 amah; beside it counted — "רחוק מן הכבש שלשה טפחים", תמיד א, ד); **the pyre** — L1 three clear unfit woods among the four, L2 four ("חוץ משל זית ושל גפן", תמיד ב, ג), L3 the air band at half its width and every log laid outside it counted (`airMiss36` — "ורוח היה בין הגזרין", תמיד ב, ד), L4 the band at 0.21 and the second pyre, of the incense: two fig logs laid beside the big one, its band 0.18 (`layer 4`, maaracha22.js `S2X/S2Z`; "וסדרו מערכה שניה של קטרת", תמיד ב, ה); **the lamb** — L1 as before ("בודקין אותו לאור האבוקות"), L2 the blemish small and only in an ear or an eye (pen25.js draws it ×0.62 — רמב״ם איסורי מזבח ב, י), L3 dirt drawn with a blemish's mark: up close both ask for the brush, the brush takes the dirt and a blemish stays ("לא יורד — מום"), L4 two lambs judged one after the other (a whole lamb approved brings "טלה שני להשוואה"), more spots (dirt up to 3, a blemish 65 %); **the inner altar** — L1 as before, L2 the gold scratches at a slower pull (vmax 2.05), L3 the gauge's accuracy hidden, L4 vmax 1.9 and the full teni carried out to the great gate held level by the right thumb (`lift36`, `teni36`: `bal36`, `E.r26bal`; a fallen coal counted — "נכנס ונטל את הטני והשתחוה ויצא", תמיד ו, א; the court's kohen no longer takes it out after him); **the menorah** — L1 the band wider ([0.88, 1.02]), L2 [0.93, 0.985] (חצי לוג), L3 the eastern lamp out every time ("מדשנן ומדליקן מן הדולקים", תמיד ג, ט), L4 [0.95, 0.97] and the western lamp out too — lit first, from a pan of the outer altar's coals by the stone (`altar`, heichal26.js `PAN36`; a fire from a lamp refused and counted — "מדליקו מעל מזבח העולה", תמיד ו, א); **the shochet** — L1 the gold [0.435, 0.565], L2 no binding guide — a binding the other way is a counted try ("ראשו לדרום ופניו למערב", תמיד ד, א), L3 [0.465, 0.535], L4 [0.482, 0.518] and the needle ×0.7 (dam27.js redraws the gold by the level); and a `//` comment typed into a one-line function of tools/autoplay35.js broke the file for ≈ 15 s while a browser job was booting (fixed at once; that job injected the fixed file and ran clean).

### build 37 — "ליטוש בנייה 36": build 36 polished from its own findings, and the laver's second spigot removed (23.10; the coordinator's brief "ליטוש בנייה 36" — no new feature; the owner's decision 23.10 06:49 "להוריד הדד"; the economy's other parts — chests, nedava, counters — untouched, docs/specs/kalkala-nedavot-he.md awaits the owner)
- **What:** (1) **The wick's swipe in any clear direction** (day33.js `r26swipe`: the wick counts when the swipe passes SWIPE36 on either axis — |Δx| ≥ 0.15 of the width or |Δy| ≥ 0.15 of the height — within ≤ 350 ms; ⬆ is still the one shown; short or slow refused as before, no "crooked" any more; the wipe keeps its ↔); heichal26.js reads the crossing time on whichever axis crosses first (`p.ta`); **a hand the first time** (`.hk26-w37`: 👆 sweeping up under a ⬆, in the right thumb's place of the verb cue, whose arrow hides meanwhile — until his first wick counts, and again after two refused in a row; no words, no source needed). The naive kid had swiped ↔ where ⬆ was asked: 1 of 17 counted at the Heichal, the menorah unfinished in 8.5 min. Holds, drags and timing windows untouched. (2) **The laver's second spigot removed** (the owner, 23.10 06:49): `C18.pads` without kiyor2, `C18.kiyorK` (its walk ×0.6) and `K20.hold2` (its 2.5 s hold) gone, the laver's stream one rate (kiyor20.js) — the laver the same for everyone; no pad in the court (`pads18` under E35: none — the axe and the basket were already hidden); **`refund37(save)`** (day33.js): a save that owns kiyor2 gets its 40 perutot back once — `save.k2r37 = 40` the flag, kiyor2 deleted; at the mount (index.js, before anything reads the save) and in `createDay33` before `st` reads the perutot; any other save untouched; no child loses a coin. The lobby and the shop (SHOP33) never had it. (3) **The ashes, the browser against node — the cause:** the tools' balance hand, not the game. Build 36's hand took the tilt's rate over one look (Δb / Δt between two looks) and fed it forward (the skilled 0.9); in the page the looks come 0.05 or 0.1 s apart (the frames at 20 a second and the bot's 50 ms timer beat — measured: the skilled 85 % at 0.05, 12 % at 0.1; the child 91 % at 0.1, 9 % at 0.05), and that jitter turned into a counter swinging ±1 every look — the pan rocked (the debug trace, `d21dbg`: u +1, −1, +1 within 0.15 s) and spilt twice (q 0.35): the skilled 0/10 at level 4 where node, looking every 0.05 s exactly, made 30 %. Not the cause: the input's delay — `E.d21bal` patched in the page showed the pad's counter reaches the engine at the same `K.T` as the look, before the next tick; not the scoop sides (the same 15 % / 2 % misreads in both); not the heap's mark (the skilled walks to 0.4 of it in both; one tap beside it in build 36's ten skilled browser runs); not the swipe gate. The child's 10/10 at level 1 was the same hand's other face: its gain is capped low (1.74) and a 0.1 s look is what node used, so it rarely rocked — and with the old pans (×0.65, spilling at 0.8) a steady hand never spilt. **The fix:** one balance hand for every balance act (the ashes' pan, the laver's stream at level 4, the teni), `hand37`, the same lines in tools/lvl36sim.mjs and tools/autoplay35.js: the tilt as seen a reaction ago (the child 0.2 s, the skilled 0.1 s), read between his looks by interpolation, its rate over a fixed 0.1 s less his own counter of then; the node sim looks at the page's intervals (`look37`). With it, and the pans retuned, the two agree within their samples (the ashes: the child at level 1 node 75 %, the browser 5/6; the skilled at level 4 node 33 %, the browser 3/6 — build 36: 10/10 and 0/10). **Then the pans retuned** (lvl36.js): level 1 ×1.2 of build 35's pan spilling at 0.68 (was ×0.65 at 0.8 — "גחלים ברורות" still: no inner side, no mark), level 2 the same pan, level 3 ×1.45 at 0.6 (was 1.4 / 0.62), level 4 ×1.65 at 0.53 (was 1.6 / 0.55); and the other two balance acts, which the steadier hand made easy (the skilled 86 % and 79 % at level 4): the laver's level-4 stream ×1.55 with its edge at 0.5 (was 1.1 / 0.55), the teni ×1.4 at 0.5 (was 1.0 / 0.55; ×1.6 first: node 21 %, but the browser 0 of 14 — every page run spilt, the cause not found: the same hand, the same look intervals, the counter reaching the engine at once; ×1.4: node 33 %, the browser 2/10). (4) **The lamb:** an eye model in both tools (`miss37`, `EYE37`): a spot is missed by its drawn size — pen25's own size of it (the region's scale 0.17 / 0.2 / 0.26 × 0.62 if small × the level's `sz`) — on one logistic for both hands (slope 0.0367; the half-seen size 0.031 for the skilled, 0.099 for the child: build 36's guesses — the skilled 1 % a plain spot and 10 % a small one, the child 6 % a plain one), and a little more when the lamb shows more spots (+15 % a spot). The content per level (lvl36.js, each with its source as before): levels 1–3 a blemish on 0.8 of the lambs and up to four straws or mud (was P25's 0.5 and 2 — more to read: "בודקין אותו לאור האבוקות" kept), level 4 0.85, up to four, and its blemish half a small one's size (`sz` 0.5 — the engine's lamb and pen25's decal carry it; the level's line "בודקים שני טלאים, והמום זעיר — ובוחרים את התמים"). **How much the model limits the numbers:** wholly. The bot has no eyes; whether a spot is "seen" is a die rolled from a curve I set from build 36's guesses, so the lamb's shares measure the curve and the content's sizes and counts through it — not a child. What the content change does is real (the blemish at level 4 is drawn half as large, there are more spots on every level); what share of real children see it is unknown until children play. Build 36's content under this same eye: the child 94 % at level 1, the skilled 75 % at level 4 — the content change brought them to 83 % and 39 % (node). he went on by himself, no parent's hand); the practices finished by the kid himself: the menorah, the inner altar and the zorek at 640, the zorek at 844 (the menorah and the inner altar at 844 not finished in 6 minutes — the pour's band and the pulls); **before** (build 38, the stick, 10 runs at 640 × 296): 7 stucks, 4 parent's-hand steps, 0 page errors — the walk to the menorah each time. **From one act's end to the next act's start** (the autoplayer's child hand, whole mornings, game seconds; before = build 38's page with the bot driving the stick's way directly, after = the guided hold, the child letting go now and then): day 2 (inner) — the laver → the lot 2.5 → 2.5, the lot → his pyre 5.4 → 7.8, his pyre → the second lot 9.8 → 9.9, the second lot → the great gate 10.0 → 12.1, the gate → his act 5.2 → 5.0, Σ 32.9 → 37.3 s (the morning 79.8 → 90.2 s); day 3 (menorah) — Σ 43.8 → 48.1 s (95.4 → 93.2 s); day 4 (shochet) — Σ 30.5 → 31.8 s (73.6 → 74.8 s); day 5 (zorek) — Σ 31.8 s (101.6 s; no before); the walk and the waits 26–29 % of the guided mornings (the spec's ≤ 25 % — at its edge, as build 36's 26 %); 0 stuck in the guided mornings but for two waits of 3 s the bot counted (at the pen's fence before the near-arrival rule; at the zorek's corner while his first gift waits for the five lamps). **Every built role by the guided walk** (the autoplayer's practice, child, level 1): the laver, the ashes, the pyre, the lamb, the inner altar, the shochet מהודר; the menorah and the zorek כשר; 0 page errors; the lamb chamber by the kid — the lamb inspected, its end card.
- **Verified by:** node tests; through `brun` only: the probe (`scratchpad/b39/probe39.cjs`, 640 × 296 and 844 × 390: the walk's surface, the stick's elements hidden, a hold of 1.5 s walking him 9 amot on the guide's way, released → 0.4 amah of ease-out, held again → to the pyre's place and its act; 0 page errors), the autoplayer's whole mornings (days 2–5 guided; days 2–4 on build 38's page as the before), its practice of all eight roles and of the zorek four times, the naive kid fifteen times (above) and once more in the lamb chamber. PNGs, each opened: no stick on the screen while he walks — `auto/b34-K39-P-d1/shot-04-f-cut.png` (640 × 296: the gold path, the hero on it, no stick, no ghost), `auto/b34-K39-P-zor/shot-03-f-covered.png` (640 × 296, the zorek's practice), `auto/b34-K39-W-zor/shot-02-scr-_run_.png` (844 × 390, at the rings); the probe's held walk — `scratchpad/b39/probe/640x296-walk39.png`, `844x390-walk39.png` (the follow camera close behind him by the Ulam's wall — the camera's own, as before)
