### build 40 — touch the thing itself, and the act's camera (26.10; the owner, 26.10 22:19: "איפה נכנסת הקביעה החדשה שבמקום לחצנים נלחץ על הפריט במסך בעצמו?"; docs/specs/hachraot-he.md "נוגעים בחפץ עצמו — במקום כפתורים", input-protocol-he.md "עדכון 26.10.2026 (ב)", shlav-alef-bimui-he.md §1–2; Phase B; the new module `src/avodah/shift/touch40.js`)
- **What:** (1) **The act on the thing** (touch40.js `createTouch40`): each act's own input layer — until now the right half of the screen (`.d21-in`, `.m22-in`, `.hk26-in`, `.dm27-in`, `.p25-in`, `.p23-in`) — is laid over its object on the screen every frame: the object's parts projected (`box40`), + 30 px each side, never under 96 px square, kept on the screen (`hit40`); the gesture each act already had (the sweep, the balance, the sort's swipe, the stack's tap, the bolt, the key's hold, the pull, the wick, the pour, the bind, the cut, the stir, the pull-back throw, the base's swipe, the torch over the lamb, the raised finger) starts where the finger lands on the thing and keeps the finger wherever it goes (pointer capture, as before); the layer never moves under a finger; off the thing the touch falls to the world — the walk's surface (build 39). The things: the ashes — the coals' bed, then the pan where his hand holds it; the pyre — the log he sorts, the layer he stacks; the House — the gate's bolt and key, the inner altar's ashes, the lamp he tends (with the lit lamp or the outer altar's pan the fire comes from), the teni; the blood — the lamb's legs to bind, its neck to cut and to catch, the vessel (while he carries it: his whole figure with it, r 1.7), the base's hole; the lamb — its eight places (`at25`); the lot — his raised hand. A soft dashed ring on the thing; a hand (👆, no text — build 37's wick lesson) the first time at each act (`bh_t40_hand`) and after two touches in a row on the world that walked nowhere. (2) **The work pad only as the fallback** (`fallback40`): the thing's footprint under 44 px, its middle off the screen, or the player's switch — the pause card's new "✋ משטח גדול" (prefs28 `pad40HTML`, `bh_pad40`, 44 px like its neighbours; not on the opening screen); then the layer is the old right half (`.t40-pad`). The work button itself was already hidden during every role's act. The laver's two rings untouched (the approved two-thumb exception). (3) **The act's camera** (twin.js `pan40`, `CAM40`): an act's authored view (camWork's `{ p, t }`) is slid sideways — the camera and its look together, at most 6 amot — so the thing stands at 60 % of the width (the lamb 55 %: the stamps תמים / פסול stand at the corner), stepped back along its look up to 8 amot while he would leave the frame; never into a wall (the slide shortened while cam28's seg cuts it and the authored view's line is clear); held exactly as it was while a finger is on the thing (`globalThis.__T40.down`); the authored view's own easing (2.4/s) moves it between steps. (4) `GAME_BUILD` 40. (5) The tools: the naive kid's act touches (kidpage.js `T40`: from an act's input appearing to its first touch; where each thumb lands — the thing, the pad, the laver's rings, the world; kid.cjs "== touch40"; a child starts the act's gesture on the thing he sees, `.t40-obj`, and half his random right-hand touches land on it); the autoplayer's one shot per surface with a thumb drawn where it lands (`shots30 t40`) and its notes (the thing's share of the width, the hit area, each fall to the pad and its reason — `globalThis.__T40S`).
- **Numbers:** **914 tests (904 pass, 10 skipped, 0 fail)** — `test/build40.test.mjs` 7 (the box, the hit area, the fallback's reasons; the layer on the thing, frozen under the finger, the pad when small or by the switch, off as it was, the hand first and after two misses; `pan40` — the thing at 60 %, him seen, the step back, the cap — and the twin's freeze; the wall guard as build 39 had it; every act's layer on its thing and destroyed with it, the laver untouched; the switch in the pause card only; GAME_BUILD 40 and the Name scan by code points); build28's hud regex and the GAME_BUILD pins (build32/33/34/37/38/39) with comments; `qa-content-scan` 12/12; `dist/temple3d.html` **5,656,360 B** (+10,653 over 39; under 6 MiB by 635,096); out/index.html "משמרת 45 (בנייה 40) — בית המקדש השני" (5,657,072 B). **The naive kid** (5 minutes a run, same seeds before and after; days 1–3 and the zorek and the inner altar): **before** (build 39's page, 5 runs at 640 × 296 and 844 × 390): 14 acts, **264 touches in the acts — every one on the right-hand pad**, 0 parent's hand, 0 stuck, 0 page errors, the first touch on an act's input 2.2 s (median of 12); **after** (build 40's pages, 8 runs): 32 acts, **203 touches on the thing, 14 on the pad** (9 + 3 in the zorek runs before the carried vessel grew to his figure, 2 on the lamb zoomed past the screen), 0 parent's hand, 0 hard stuck, one 60 s stall on the opening screen's journal (not an act), 0 page errors, the first touch 1.9 s (median of 29); on the final page's zorek run 17 touches on the thing, 0 on the pad. **Every built role by the autoplayer's child hand** (level 1, 0 page errors): 640 × 296 — the laver, the lamb, the shochet מהודר; the ashes, the pyre, the inner altar כשר; the menorah כשר and מהודר; the zorek כשר (three runs); 844 × 390 — the laver, the ashes, the lamb, the inner altar, the menorah, the shochet, the zorek מהודר; the pyre כשר. The thing's middle at 59–61 % of the width in every shot of an act with an authored view (the zorek's carry 43 %, the lamb 51–60 %).
- **Verified by:** node tests; through `brun` only: the kid 13 times, the autoplayer's practice of all eight roles at both sizes (four roles a job) and of the zorek and the menorah three more times, build 39's probe twice more for the follow camera. PNGs, each opened (the thumb drawn where it lands): `auto/b40-pr-P1/shot-t40-d21.png` (the coals' bed, the hand, him on the left), `shot-t40-d21pan.png`, `shot-t40-m22.png`, `shot-t40-m22stack.png`, `shot-t40-p25.png`; `auto/b40-pr-P2/shot-t40-hk26.png` (the menorah), `shot-t40-hk26alt.png`, `shot-t40-hk26gate.png`, `shot-t40-dm27cut.png`, `shot-t40-dm27aim.png`; `auto/b40-pr-W1/shot-t40-d21.png`, `shot-t40-p25.png`; `auto/b40-pr-W2/shot-t40-dm27bind.png` (the shochet), `shot-t40-dm27recv.png` (the carry), `shot-t40-hk26alt.png`; the pause card with the switch `auto/b34-K40A-P-d3/shot-03-scr-_sh_pause_run_paused_.png`.
- **What remains:** **the follow camera hugging his back by a wall is not fixed** — two tries in this build were measured worse and reverted (a rise to 0.5 with a lifted pull: at 640 × 296 he fell out of the frame's foot, the look point being ahead of him, at 844 × 390 it looked down onto his head — `scratchpad/b40/probe/`; a swing round him at full distance: both frames stood in a wall's face — `scratchpad/b40/probeB/`); cam28.js is as build 39 had it; the way on: the look point back onto him while the guard pulls, then the rise; the practice's title card ("אימון — …") covers the kohen for its 2 s at an act's start; the lamb's zoom (pen25's macro) leaves the kohen out of the frame; the sort's authored view keeps him right of the log, not in the left third; the inner altar's ring sits on his shoulder and the altar's top together; the first touch on the thing at the zorek's first frame can fall to the pad while the camera arrives (footprint 37 px for 1 s); the stations' timing acts (salt, wood …) keep their own zone on the thing and the work button beside it (the button not removed there); the stamps תמים / פסול and the lot's cards are choices, still buttons; the old browser touch tests (`twin-touch.cjs`, `qa-walk.cjs`) not run; the sounds never heard.
- **Deviations:** (1) the hit area is the object's box + 30 px and at least 96 px (the brief's ≥ 44 px; the finger rules' 88 for the pad); (2) a thing in his hands is his hands with it (the pan, the teni, the vessel — while he carries the blood his whole figure), so it stays over 44 px in the follow camera's far frame; (3) the camera's work is a slide of each act's authored view (no new view per act; the stations' own views untouched); (4) the follow camera's wall fix not delivered (above); (5) the kid's act gesture starts on `.t40-obj` when he sees one — the same kid on both pages; (6) **rule lapse:** once, a syntax check by `node -e "require('tools/kid34/kid.cjs')"` ran the kid outside `brun` for about 13 s (Chromium launched; it ended with the command, the pipe closed); every other browser job through `brun` with `run_in_background`, no shell `&`, no process listed or killed.

### build 41 — the camera and the framing (27.10; the coordinator's build-41 brief: finish what build 40 left open — the follow camera by walls, the act views too close, the practice's title card over him, the lamb's macro without him, the inner altar's ring, the zorek's fall to the pad while the camera arrives, the timing acts' work button)
- **What:** (1) **The follow camera by a wall** (cam28 `WALL41`): blocked under the shift's pull-only rule, before the old pull the guard takes a *readable* pose about his head — first at the camera's own elevation, then risen in steps to 55°; each at its own distance and then nearer down to 9.5 amot (his 3.9-amah figure at the shift's 50° view stays ≤ 45 % of the height); then a swing round him (±0.35, ±0.7 rad) only if its way there is clear (checked at a third and two thirds — build 40's swing stood in a wall's face mid-way); always with room 1.2 amot behind the camera (never at a wall's face); while such a pose or the pull holds, the look comes back onto his middle (1.9 over his feet), eased at 3/s, and the camera is turned to it in the same frame (the guard now gets the camera); the old pull only when nothing clears; the glide is the guard's own (≤ 50 amot/s): never a cut; the search budget a parameter (`o.budget`, default 9 ms). (2) **Every act's view** (twin.js `pan40` with `CAM41`, `HERO41` 3.96 — the model's measured height): besides the thing at 55–65 % of the width, his whole figure ≤ 45 % of the screen's height and inside the frame on both sides, stepping back up to 14 amot; a step a wall cuts is first raised over it (2, 4, 6 amot), then shortened; the views without a thing (the laver, the lot) only step back; still frozen while a finger is on the thing. (3) **The practice's title** a slim line in the top band's right corner (hud `intro(…, slim)`), never over him. (4) **The lamb** (day33 `P41.lamb`): the scan from higher and farther (el ≥ 0.72, d ≥ 7.5 — over the pen's walls, him whole), the macro from the lamb's far side from him, 3.4 amot and a little wider (pen25 `ZOOM_FOV` 28, was 20). (5) **The inner altar's ring** on the altar's own top (`GOLD_ALTAR`, was his stand). (6) **The fall to the work pad** after the camera's move (touch40 `T41`): never in an act's first second, and only once the thing has stayed small or off the screen 1 s in a row; back on the thing only 8 px past the 44 px rule. (7) **The timing acts** (salt, wood …): the work button hidden while their zone is on the thing (stations `s41-zone`; the player's switch keeps it). (8) Walking with the blood the thing is his whole figure (r 2.0 round his middle). (9) `GAME_BUILD` 41. (10) The tools: `tools/cam41.js` — every 50 ms his figure's share of the screen's height (feet to hat, the model's bounds), whole in the frame or not, the place ahead on the screen, by kind of frame ('act': an authored view eased in; 'walk'; 'still'); `autoplay35-run --cam41` prints it per role and shoots each tight spot.
- **Numbers:** **920 tests (910 pass, 10 skipped, 0 fail)** — `test/build41.test.mjs` 6 (WALL41's numbers against the 18–45 % band; a wall behind him: the readable pose ≥ 9.5 amot, ≤ 55°, clear with room behind, the look on his middle, the camera turned — no wall, nothing changes; the menorah's case: build 40's rule left him over 45 %, build 41's steps back to ≤ 45 % with the thing still at 60 %; T41's settle and hysteresis; the slim title, the altar's ring, the timing acts' button; GAME_BUILD 41 and the Name scan); `cam28.test`'s pull-only case accepts the readable pose (comment); build40's tests (the clock for T41, GOLD_ALTAR, the generalized view) and the GAME_BUILD pins with comments; `qa-content-scan` 12/12; `dist/temple3d.html` **5,659,560 B** (under 6 MiB by 631,896); out/index.html "משמרת 45 (בנייה 41) — בית המקדש השני" (5,660,272 B). **The camera, measured** (every role's practice by the autoplayer's child hand, 640 × 296 and 844 × 390, build 40's page against build 41's, ~15,000 samples each side): **walking** — over 45 % of the height 18.6 % → 8.1 % of the samples, not whole in the frame 7.6 % → 2.7 %, the largest 1.35 → 0.87 of the height; **in the acts** — over 45 % 53.7 % → 3.4 %, not whole in the frame 54.2 % → 6.1 %, the largest 1,498 (the lamb's macro) → 0.80; standing 15.9 % → 2.6 %; the place ahead off the screen while walking 25 % → 40 % (the look on him, not ahead). **By the Ulam's wall** (build 39's probe walk, both sizes): the largest share walking 5.26 / 7.44 → 0.89 / 1.09 of the height; standing there afterwards, over 45 % in 263 / 250 samples → 25 / 25 — but his figure still not whole in the frame there (209 / 210 samples): no readable pose clears that spot and the old pull stays. **All roles** (autoplayer, child, level 1, 0 page errors): 640 — laver, ashes, pyre, lamb, inner altar, menorah, shochet מהודר, zorek כשר (twice); 844 — laver, ashes, pyre, lamb, inner altar, menorah, shochet, zorek מהודר. **The naive kid** (5 runs a side, same seeds; build 41's runs on its pages of the evening): touches on the thing 115 → 116, on the pad 2 → 2, parent's hand 0 → 0, stucks 1 (the lobby's journal) → 0, page errors 0 → 0, the first touch on an act 1.9 → 1.2 s (median).
- **Verified by:** node tests; through `brun` only: the practices with the measure (before ×4, after ×4, the lamb ×2, the zorek and the ashes again), the walk probe ×3 (before, after, after the swing). PNGs, each opened: walking by tight spots — `auto/c41A-P1/shot-cam41-1.png` (to the laver, risen), `shot-cam41-4.png` (the pyre's run, risen, the gold path ahead), `auto/c41A-P2/shot-cam41-3.png` (the zorek, risen), `shot-cam41-4.png` (the altar's corner, pulled — 57 %); the Ulam's wall `scratchpad/b41/probeB/844x390-walk39.png` (before) and `probeA/844x390-walk39.png`, `probeA/640x296-walk39.png` (after — still too close); the acts — `auto/c41A-P2/shot-t40-hk26.png` and `auto/c41A-W2/shot-t40-hk26.png` (the menorah: him whole, ~45 %), `auto/c41A-P2/shot-t40-hk26alt.png` (the ring on the altar's top), `auto/c41A-W2/shot-t40-hk26gate.png` (the slim title at the top right), `auto/c41A-lambP/shot-t40-p25.png` (the lamb from above, him in the frame).
- **What remains:** the Ulam's south wall (x −52, z 24): no readable pose and no clear swing — the pull stays and his figure is cut there (the probe's shots: his hat from above); the altar's corners — the pull up to 57 % now and then; the place ahead is off the screen more often while walking (the look on him); the ashes' pan on the way down falls to the pad at 640 × 296 after its second (38 px; r 2.0 round his middle was measured worse — his figure out of the descent's frame — and reverted); the lamb from above reads his hat more than his face, and some of the macro's frames still large; in the acts he is under 18 % more often (498 of 12,550 samples) — the steps back; the old browser touch tests not run; the sounds never heard.
- **Deviations:** (1) the readable pose's distances are the measured band's (≥ 9.5 amot for ≤ 45 %), the swing comes after every pose at his own bearing; (2) the act rule steps back (and rises over walls) rather than re-authoring each view; the lamb's views re-authored (P41.lamb); (3) the macro keeps him by its angle and a wider field, no inset; (4) the measure counts a figure not whole in the frame as "out" with its height unknown; (5) reverted in this build: the ashes' pan as his whole figure (r 2.0) — measured worse; (6) **rule lapses:** none — every browser job through `brun` with `run_in_background`, syntax checks by `node --check` only, no shell `&`, no process listed or killed.

### build 42 — the new Phase A, stage 01 "שיטוח ההר", end to end (27.10; the coordinator's build-42 brief: a vertical slice — the input of Phase B, the shared families engine, the stage from the flyover to one card, the materials by day, the haptic library, the camera rules; the owner, 27.10 01:58: maximal efficiency without hurting quality)
- **What:** (1) **The families engine** — `src/game/fam42.js` (pure, no DOM): LIFT (the treadwheel: held > 0.25 s it walks, let go it coasts with momentum; over the window a drag lowers it — any way, and left alone 3 s the crew's brake lets it sink back into the window as a correction, so it never stays stuck; stopped short is no fault — lift again), PLACE (the course's hand stones of 2/3/4 with staggered joints — a joint over a joint below is a fault, a stone that would leave a gap no stone fills is refused — and a still hold to settle; the great stone on rollers to the chant's beat, the freed roller dragged back → front, מהודר = never a gap; the last paving slabs 1/2/3 that fill each row exactly), INSPECT (the light swept across the rock shows the bedding line only near its grazing angle; wedges along it — at level 1 the even places are marked and a tap on the rock lands on the nearest free one; the water held and let go as the crack reaches the end — let go early, pour again, no fault; poured past the end is the mistake; rock 3 has two lines, one with the layers). Grades through rate30 (מהודר / כשר / טעון תיקון), levels 1–4 per family by lvl36's rule (two מהודר open the next), a retry free, never a loss. **Taps alone never finish a job** (a tap < 0.25 s or a drag < 24 px does nothing; test). (2) **The stage** — `src/game/har42.js`: the flyover (first time 16 s from over the Mount of Olives, the gold plan unrolling, a slow orbit, the dive; later 3 s with a skip), the rope walked between the pegs, the wheel, three rocks (clear, faint, two lines), two courses where his hand lays 1–3 stones while the crews lay the rest (a pull-back at each course's end), the great stone on rollers, the last paving (its rows' empty beds inside the paving already laid, the row's gap outlined), "לבית התקיעה" traced along the dots, the reveal (the crane up, the opening's rock before/after, in the stage's dusk light) and ONE card. The walk is build 39's guide (hold anywhere → he walks to the next act; a drag chooses), the touch on the thing build 40's touch40, the act's frame build 41's pan40/CAM41 (side-on, frozen under the finger, him ≤ 45 % of the height, the thing near his right hand). Gone for stage 01: the measure/dig taps, the paid finishing ("🪙 לשלם"), the separate check, the card chain, the "steel tools" card. (3) **The materials by day** (v2 §9): a caravan each morning (24 units: a whole stage 01 fits one day), kept up to 3 days, checked only at a part's start; out → the card "מחר: …", no wait-or-pay; this replaces the "priests' training 150 min" gate for stage 01; dev flags `?h42day=`, `?h42stock=`, `?h42at=<part>`. (4) **The haptic library** (hishaarut-ve-retet-he.md §1.2) next to prefs28's switch: `HAPTIC42` + `buzz42` (the switch respected, ≤ 2 s a minute). (5) **Wiring** — `src/game/index.js`: while the Mount is not done (not the demo, not `?har42=0` / `?stick=1`), a new game and a returning one enter the new stage 01; the old Mount's flow, its opening and the twin step aside; its end completes the Mount (`S.completeStage(G,'har')`, ftue 99) and stage 02 onward play as before. GAME_BUILD 42 (the pins in build32–41's tests updated with comments). `tools/mkout.cjs --no-shift`. Every new line "טיוטה" + its source or "המחשה"; touch targets ≥ 44 px; RTL; עולי רגל. The tools: `tools/auto42.js` + `auto42-run.cjs` (the stage's autoplayer, a child's hand or the quick one), the naive kid's mode `mount` (`tools/kid34/kidmodes.cjs`; its parent's hand is auto42's quick hand after 75 s without progress) and `--query`.
- **Found by the runs and fixed in this build** (each its own commit): the paving could not be dropped (its box from two corners, one behind the camera's 4-amot near plane → null; the autoplayer stalled there at both sizes); the paving's stand moved east (from the south the act's camera stood behind the crews' course); the reveal and the card had gone dark (after the Mount completes the real clock's night came — now the stage's dusk until the card, the real night after it); **a course could dead-end** (a stone leaving 1 unit — the kid stood there; now refused, "gap"); **the freed roller's handle took no finger** (under the stage's pointer-events-none layer a touch fell through to the walk — the autoplayer's synthetic events had hidden it; the kid found it at both sizes); **every mark of the acts was drawn 340 px off and clipped** (the svg layer with inset 0 alone is 300 × 150 at its corner — the rock's lines, wedges and crack, the course's slot and joints, the paving's gap, the inscription's dots; a probe found it); **the stage's views were rendered from 35 amot** (main.js's OrbitControls floor, near plane 4 — the follow view's 17 and the act's 12 were pushed back after the stage set them, so the picture was farther than its frame and the marks and touch rings landed 30–90 px off their things; a probe measured it: the paving's centre projected at (410, 105), drawn at ≈ (332, 118)) — the stage now takes `view.closeCam(true)` while it is on, as Phase B's close-up does; the wheel over the window (the kid lifted it again and again: now any drag lowers it there, and the brake); the kid harness itself (its stuck watch: through lookRec, and on the act's progress, not every move; it holds when the line says החזיקו; the waiting stone's line).
- **Numbers:** **929 tests (919 pass, 10 skipped, 0 fail)** — `test/build42.test.mjs` 9 (taps alone never finish the wheel, the rock, the course, the rollers or the paving — a hundred taps of every kind; LIFT, INSPECT, PLACE/ROLL/PAVE grades incl. the course's gap rule over 300 seeds and the wheel's brake; the levels by lvl36's rule; the materials by day; the haptic library; the wiring incl. each runs' fix; every line with its source or המחשה, ≥ 44 px, GAME_BUILD 42, no Divine Name by code points); **qa 12/12**; **dist 5,703,023 B (5.44 MiB) ≤ 6 MiB**; out/index.html 5,703,622 B. **The autoplayer's child hand** (rough timing, now and then a wrong choice; from a new game): the whole stage at both sizes — 147–157 s on its clock, 0 page errors, the Mount completed (ftue 99) and stage 02 next ("חומת העזרה"); **מהודר at level 1: 20 of 22** (lift 4/4, inspect 8/8, place 8/10; a42-W2 844, a42-P6/P7/P8 640) — above the 60–80 % target; its hands-on ≈ 1.6 min. With the camera at its own distance (230e33d) the same hand: a42-P9 the whole stage in 156 s, 0 page errors, level 1 5/6. **The naive kid** (reads the line and tries, wanders, never waits; its parent's hand after 75 s without progress): from a new game in 8.5 min at 844 (K42-W-new5) it reached the inscription (part 10 of 12) with 1 parent's hand, 0 hard stucks, 0 page errors, hands-on 7.1 min to there (the whole stage ≈ 9–10 min); at 640 (K42-P-new6) and again at 844 with the near camera (K42-W-new6) it reached the second course (part 7), 3 parent's hands each — all at the course's settle-hold (it drops anywhere on the screen; the slot's outline was invisible until the svg fix), hands-on 6.2 / 5.0 min to there; from the great stone (K42-P-roll2) it finished the stage, the reveal and the card in 3.6 min (1 parent's hand at the inscription); **מהודר at level 1 for the naive kid: 9 of 26** (lift 0/4, inspect 3/11, place 6/11 over K42-W-new5, K42-W-new6, K42-P-new5, K42-P-new6, K42-P-roll2) — below the target; the target lies between the two hands.
- **Verified by:** node tests; through `brun` only (each run its own job): the autoplayer a42-P2…P11 and a42-W1/W2 (640 × 296, 844 × 390), the naive kid K42-* ×15 (both sizes, a new game and the great stone's fixture `?h42at=roll`), three probes of the paving (the null box, the svg's 300 × 150, the camera's floor). PNGs opened: `auto/a42-P5/shot-reveal.png` (the reveal in daylight), `auto/a42-P5/shot-card.png` (the one card: מהודר 7 · כשר 2, ידיים 2 דק׳, נפתח: חומת העזרה), `auto/a42-P7/shot-act-course.png` (the slot outlined and the joints below in red), `auto/a42-P8/shot-act-trace.png` (the dots on the parapet), `auto/a42-P8/shot-act-pave.png` (the beds and the gap's outline — still off before the camera fix), `auto/probe-pave/p1.png`, `auto/a42-W2/shot-open.png` (the flyover); with the camera at its own distance `auto/a42-P11/shot-act-rock.png` (the touch ring on the rock), `shot-act-course.png` (the slot and the red joints on the course), `shot-act-pave.png` (the gap's outline over the row, still a little off its bed).
- **What remains:** (1) the naive kid's level-1 מהודר is low (9/26) and the autoplayer's high (20/22) — no real child measured; the levels' numbers are first guesses; (2) the naive kid's main stall is the course's settle-hold (it drops anywhere; a real child sees the slot now) — next: accept a drop anywhere over the course's stretch, not only its slot box and the great stone's handle (it was untouchable until afb5e92 — measured once after: K42-P-roll2 finished); (3) the crowbar-flick for the giant stones is not in stage 01 (PLACE's twist is the rollers); (4) the props are simple shapes (המחשה) — no models; the flyover's gold plan is four gold edges unrolling; (5) **his figure in the act views is ~65–70 % of a 640 × 296 height** with the camera at its own distance (a42-P9…P11) — the act's distance (12 → 18, then a 16-amot floor after pan40: 9f853e4, bd4e468) changed nothing in the picture, so something after the stage's frame sets the rendered pose (most likely cam28's `view.guard`, which main.js runs after the game's frame) — not resolved; before 230e33d the 35-amot floor showed him small but every mark and touch ring off its thing; (6) the act shots are taken 3 s after an act begins, under the swiftshader clock; (7) the browser's close after a finished stage takes ≈ 5 minutes under swiftshader (the old Phase A's night scene) — not looked into.
- **Deviations:** (1) the materials: 24 units a morning cover the whole stage 01 (no "מחר" in a first sitting; the dev flags test it); (2) "hand stones per course 1–3": the stretch is 6 units (7 from level 3) of stones of 2/3/4 — 2 or 3 stones; (3) the reveal's "before" is the opening's own frame (a snapshot), not a separate render; (4) the old Mount stage's flow is kept behind `?har42=0` / `?stick=1` (the stick is not in the new stage); (5) level-1 aids were added from the kid's runs: the wheel stopped short is no fault, over the window any drag lowers and the brake lets it sink back; the rock's wedge places marked and a tap snaps to the nearest free one; the water let go early is no fault.
- **How out/index.html was made:** `node build.mjs` then `node tools/mkout.cjs "בנייה 42 — שיטוח ההר — בית המקדש השני" --no-shift` — no `__FORCE_SHIFT` head script (the bundle's own reader of the flag is the only occurrence), so Phase A opens and a new game enters the new stage 01.
- **Rule lapse:** one — at 02:10 UTC a queue script (`runs42c.sh`: kid ×4, autoplayer ×1, all through `brun`) was started with a shell `&` (a chained command's tail) instead of `run_in_background`; it ran to its end through the queue. Also: three of this build's own queue scripts were stopped with the session's task-stop while a browser job of theirs was running (to rerun on a fixed page, 01:21, 02:07, 02:09 UTC); no process was listed or killed by hand, and the queue's lock freed itself each time.

### build 43 — stage 01 polished to the owner's bar (27.10; the coordinator's build-43 brief, the owner: "מרהיב… שיתרשם מהגודל של ההר… ובסוף ייראה משטח חלק"; nine items, in their order)
- **What:** (1) **The act's camera.** Nothing overrode the stage's pose after its frame (the rendered camera is the stage's own: 0.00 amot apart in every act frame measured). Build 42 framed for Phase B's kohen (twin.js HERO41 3.96 amot) while Phase A's figure is **6.44 amot** (measured in the page), so pan40 stopped too near. The stage now measures his figure once (`heroHeight`: the visible meshes of the stages' hero; 6.4 if it cannot), gives pan40 that height, and keeps the camera back along the view's ray to the distance at which he is ≤ 45 % (0.42 with a margin); the follow camera likewise; frozen under the finger as before (`src/game/har42.js` actView/followView). The measure: `tools/cam41.js` on the stage (his feet and height from `__H42.heroBox`, the thing's place across the width, the rendered camera against the stage's). (2) **The reveal**: from the last slab the crane rises to the opening's own viewpoint over the Mount of Olives (`VIEWS.olives`), the whole smooth platform in the late sun (`timeOfDay('golden')`; the real clock again after the card), and there the opening's rock (its first frame, taken before the plan unrolls) crossfades in for a moment and out — 9.5 s, a skip. (3) **The props**: rocks of displaced geometry with tex.js's rock map; every ashlar (the corner stone, the courses, the parapet, the great stone — now 12 amot) with a drafted margin and a raised boss on both faces; the treadwheel a wooden drum of two rims, 30 boards and spokes on its axle in an A-frame, with a mast, a jib and the rope down to the stone; wooden wedges driven into the rock's line; the paving in slabs with their joints; all procedural (dist +10 KB). (4) **One short instruction and a small source chip** (the line's text before " · " in bold, one line; after it the chip); the long sources moved to the card (`SRC42`). (5) **The crowbar flick** (v2 §3) for the giant corner stone, a new part after the wheel (`corner`): the crane lowers it a little short of its place; a flick of the lever is a drag on the stone — its length is the push (fam42 `FLICK42`/`createFlick`), toward or away by its way on the screen; past the window the other side is an overshoot (a fault; nudge it back); level 1 shows a ghost of the place; a tap moves nothing. A build-42 save past the wheel moves on by one. (6) **The course's drop**: a stone let go anywhere over his stretch goes to the free place nearest the release (`createCourse` place({u})); his choice of stone and place stays his — a joint over a joint is counted, never corrected; a place that would leave a gap no stone fills is refused; each empty place is outlined on the course. (7) **The paving's gap** outlined on its row (the four corners projected, not a box). (8) **The card's source**: "הר הבית היה חמש מאות אמה על חמש מאות אמה" (מדות ב, א); the House's snow (War 5.222–223) is gone. (9) **Phase B**, the follow camera by a building's wall: when the guard would pull (no readable pose, no clear swing) and the block is a building of the stages alone (not the ground, an extra box or the smoke), the pose stays and the wall between is dithered by the see-through (`cam28` mode `xray`, `stages.xrayAt`, batch.js XRAY) — not measured in a browser. GAME_BUILD 43 (the pins of build32–42's tests moved with comments).
- **Numbers:** **The act's camera, cam41 on stage 01 (the autoplayer's run, every act frame sampled):** at 640 × 296 — build 42's page: his figure 59.6 % of the height on average, over 45 % in 1,595 of 1,706 act frames (93 %), not whole in the frame in 222 (13 %); build 43 (c43-dev3): 36.2 % on average, over 45 % in 0, not whole in 0, under 18 % in 71 (4 %); the thing at 59 % of the width on average, outside 50–70 % in 67 frames (3 %: the corner stone at 70–71 %, the first rock at 43–48 %); the rendered camera 0.00 amot from the stage's; the camera 27.6 amot from him. Walking: over 45 % 523 of 1,272 → 0 of 1,313. 844 × 390 — build 42's page: 59.6 % on average, over 45 % in 1,872 of 1,981 (94 %), not whole in 252 (13 %); build 43 (a43-W, the final page): 36.7 % on average, over 45 % 0 of 1,692, not whole 0, the thing at 59.3 % (outside 50–70 % in 30, 1.8 %: the corner stone at 71 %, the rocks at 38–49 %), the camera 27.1 amot from him; walking over 45 % 678 of 1,631 → 0 of 1,790; the whole stage in 244 s, 0 page errors, level 1 5 of 5 מהודר. The final page at 640 × 296 (a43-P): 36.8 % on average, over 45 % 0 of 1,742, not whole 0, the thing at 59.3 % (outside 50–70 % in 26 frames, 1.5 %), the camera 27.0 amot from him; the whole stage in 237 s on the bot's clock, 0 page errors, the Mount completed, every grade מהודר (level 1: 5 of 5, the corner stone included). **937 tests (927 pass, 10 skipped, 0 fail)** — `test/build43.test.mjs` 8 (the flick: taps never move it, a flick as long as the gap sets it home, too strong overshoots and a nudge back sets it, past the cap two flicks, level 1's ghost; the course: a release anywhere goes to the nearest free place, a joint over a joint counted, a gap refused, 300 seeds of random stones and places — every course ends, taps never place; the corner after the wheel and a build-42 save moved on by one; the lines short with their chips and the card's Mount source; the reveal; the props; Phase B's dither; GAME_BUILD 43 and the Name scan); **qa 12/12**; **dist 5,713,157 B (5.45 MiB) ≤ 6 MiB**; out/index.html 5,713,756 B. **The naive kid on the final page** (8.5 min from a new game): at 640 × 296 (K43-P-new) and at 844 × 390 (K43-W-new) it reached the paving (part 11 of 13), 3 parent's hands each (the corner stone once — an overshoot it did not nudge back at 844 —, the course's settle-hold twice), 0 hard stucks, 0 page errors, hands-on 6.8 / 7.0 min to there (the whole stage ≈ 8–9 min); level-1 מהודר 1 of 8 and 2 of 7; from the great stone (K43-P-roll) it finished the stage, the reveal and the card in 3.6 min with 1 parent's hand (the inscription), 0 page errors.
- **Verified by:** node tests; through `brun` only, every browser job run in the background: the camera's before on build 42's page (c43-before-P4 640, c43-before-W4 844), the development runs c43-dev1…3, the final page's autoplayer a43-P / a43-W (both with the camera's measure), the naive kid K43-P-new, K43-W-new, K43-P-roll. PNGs opened (`/tmp/claude-0/-home-claude/92bf52df-7cb5-51be-8ceb-ea3690eaf336/auto/`): `a43-P/shot-open.png` (the opening over the Mount of Olives), `a43-P/shot-reveal-end.png` (the same viewpoint: the whole platform in the late sun, "אחרי — משטח חלק"), `a43-P/shot-act-corner.png` (the corner stone by the treadwheel, the touch ring on it, him about 45 %), `a43-P/shot-act-rock.png` (a real rock right of him, the touch ring on it), `a43-P/shot-act-course.png` (the empty place outlined on the course, the joints below in red), `a43-P/shot-act-pave.png` (the paving in slabs, the gap outlined on its row), `a43-P/shot-card.png` (the one card and its sources), `c43-dev3/shot-act-lift.png` (the wooden wheel, jib and rope).
- **What remains:** (1) the old flow's next-target marker (a gold beam and outline for חומת העזרה) shows in the reveal's last seconds — the Mount is completed when the crane starts; (2) the great stone's and the courses' faces read dark in the dusk light (one rock map stretched over a 12-amah face); (3) the corner stone sits at 70–71 % of the width and the first rocks at 38–49 % (≈ 1.5–1.8 % of the act frames outside 50–70 %); (4) Phase B's dithered wall (item 9) is not measured in a browser — the guard falls back to the old pull where the stages' see-through is not available; (5) the naive kid's level-1 numbers remain a stress model, not a child's; (6) no real child has played it.
- **Deviations:** (1) the corner stone is its own part after the wheel (the crane lowers what the wheel lifted); its family is PLACE; (2) the reveal sets the late sun for its 9.5 s and gives the real clock back after the card; (3) the source chip shares the line's box (a second, smaller row) rather than a separate floating chip; (4) the props are procedural boxes, icospheres and the game's canvas textures — no models; (5) the dither for Phase B replaces only the guard's last fallback (the pull), not the readable poses before it.
- **How out/index.html was made:** `node build.mjs` then `node tools/mkout.cjs "בנייה 43 — שיטוח ההר — בית המקדש השני" --no-shift` (no head script: Phase A opens; a new game enters stage 01).
- **Rule lapse:** one — a development run (c43-dev2, 05:00 UTC) was started as a foreground command and the tool moved it to the background after 120 s, instead of `run_in_background` from the start; no shell `&`, no queue script stopped.

### build 44 — between stage 01 and stage 02: the vision, the gold button, חצר האומנים, the craftsman's test (27.10; the coordinator's build-44 brief; docs/specs/shlav-alef-v2-he.md §8, §12; shlav-alef-hard-fun-he.md §5)
- **What:** (1) **The vision**, once, right after stage 01's card: Phase A hands over (`main.js` `enterVision` → `startAvodah('shift:vision')` → Phase B's `start` skips the name and the intro → the shift's `runVision`): the flight to the finished House, "כך זה ייראה כשתסיים לבנות" (חזון · המחשה) over the court at sunrise, and ONE act — the laver (kiyor20) at its level 1, on the practice run's engine over a copy of his Phase B save (nothing of his day written), the guided walk and the touch of builds 39–40; "חזרה להר" from the 5th second; at the act's end (or 70 s) back to the Mount. A saved flag (`G.vision44` and `localStorage bh_vision44`): never again. (2) **The gold button**: the menu's gold "הצצה" and the invitation after the horns are gone for players until Chanukat HaBayit (`src/game/hooks/avodah.js` `menuLabel`, `hooks/index.js` `avodahInvite`); after Chanukat HaBayit the button opens Phase B for all, as before. **The hidden test switch (how a tester reaches Phase B now):** (a) open the page once with `?tester=1` — kept in that browser (`localStorage bh_tester44`; `?tester=0` clears it): the gold button and the altar's invitation come back; (b) `#avodah` in the address opens Phase B at once (as before); (c) a page made by `node tools/mkout.cjs "<title>"` WITHOUT `--no-shift` opens straight into Phase B's shift (the head flag `__FORCE_SHIFT`, unchanged); (d) the investors' demo keeps the button. (3) **חצר האומנים** (`har42.js` `yard44`, the stage director in mode `yard`): from the menu ("חצר האומנים", once the Mount is done) and from the test's card; every variant of stage 01 he met — the treadwheel, the crowbar flick, the course, reading the rock (the rock by the level), the rollers, the paving — at any level opened (lvl36's rule: the yard's grades open levels), no building, no materials; points per family (מהודר 3 · כשר 2 · טעון תיקון 1) and a title at 6 — מנופאי, בנאי, בודק, נגר (טיוטה; התאמה: "בקרוב"); the test's gold seal shown there. The sheet follows Phase B's roles book (rows, level chips ≥ 44 px, "עוד פעם" / "לחצר"). (4) **The craftsman's test** before chapter ב׳ (`har42.js` `test44`, mode `test`; `game/index.js` `wantedTest44`: the Mount done and the wall not begun → at Phase A's start, and right after the vision): three short jobs at level 2 — the wheel, a course, the crowbar; a job passes at כשר; מהודר in all three — the gold seal (kept; shown in the yard); tries free and without limit; the fifth failed try of a job — "רב האומנים מדגים": his ghost hand does it through the same surfaces (pointer events, a ghost circle) and the job is completed without a seal — no kid is ever stuck. The card's source line: "מן הכהנים לימד סתתות ונגרות — קדמוניות טו 390 · טיוטה". (5) **Small**: the reveal no longer shows the next stage's gold beam and outline (the stages that open are shown locked until the card); the big stones' faces lighter in the dusk (the margin's face without the rock map's blotches, a little warmth). GAME_BUILD 44.
- **Numbers:** **943 tests (933 pass, 10 skipped, 0 fail)** — `test/build44.test.mjs` 6 (the retry rule: free tries, the fifth failure → the demonstration; the gate and the seal: passed when every job passed or was shown, the seal only for מהודר in all three, a shown job no seal, the test only before chapter ב׳; the vision once — the saved flag, the shift's runVision, the skip at 5 s, 70 s at most; the gold button gone for players and back behind the test switch, the altar's invitation for testers; the yard's variants, titles and points, its way back to the test; the reveal without the next target; GAME_BUILD 44 and the Name scan); the older tests of the gold button moved behind the test switch (test/hooks.test.mjs, with comments); **qa 12/12**; **dist 5,730,285 B (5.46 MiB) ≤ 6 MiB**; out/index.html 5,730,909 B**. **The autoplayer** (a new game, 640 × 296, a44-P): stage 01 in 214 s → the card → the vision (≈ 40 s, its act begun, "חזרה להר") → the test's card → the yard (visited once) → the three jobs, each מהודר at the first try → the gold seal; 327 s in all, 0 page errors. **The naive kid from stage 01's card** (`?h42at=reveal`, 8.5 min, the final page): at 640 × 296 (K44-P3) — the vision at 0:55 (seen, its act tried), the test's card, "לחצר האומנים קודם" at 1:47 (4 runs; 12 points in הנחה — "בנאי"), back to the test at 3:45, **passed at 5:18** (the wheel כשר at its second try, the course מהודר, the crowbar כשר — no seal), 0 stucks, no parent's hand, 0 page errors; at 844 × 390 (K44-W2) — the vision at 0:56, the yard at 1:53 (2 runs), the test from 2:37, **passed at 6:18** (כשר, מהודר, כשר — no seal), 2 parent's hands (the course's settle-hold in the test), 0 hard stucks, 0 page errors. Before the yard asked back (pages before 3988d12): three runs (K44-P, K44-P2, K44-W) stayed in the yard practising to the end of their 8.5 minutes and never reached the test — hence its "למבחן" after two runs.
- **Verified by:** node tests; through `brun` only, every browser job in the background: the autoplayer a44-dev1 (from the reveal), a44-P (a new game, the whole flow), a44-P2 / a44-P3 (from the reveal, 640 and 844, the shots); the naive kid K44-P, K44-W, K44-P2 (before the yard's way back), K44-P3, K44-W2 (the final page). PNGs opened (`/tmp/claude-0/-home-claude/92bf52df-7cb5-51be-8ceb-ea3690eaf336/auto/`): `a44-P3/shot-vision.png` (the kohen at the laver in the finished court, "כך זה ייראה כשתסיים לבנות", "חזרה להר"), `a44-P3/shot-yard.png` (the six variants, the families' points, "חזרה למבחן"), `a44-P3/shot-test-card.png` (the test's card and its source line), `a44-P3/shot-test-act-course.png` (a test job: the course at level 2 on the plaza), `a44-P3/shot-seal.png` (the gold seal), `a44-P2/shot-test-act-lift.png` (the wheel on the plaza among עולי רגל).
- **What remains:** (1) the vision's act is the practice run of the laver: its flight is main.js's 2.6-s flight to the altar and the House, then the act — no longer flyover of its own; (2) the naive kid stalls at the course's settle-hold in the test (2 parent's hands at 844); (3) the yard's levels follow lvl36 — a kid from the fixture sees level 1 only; (4) "נגר" waits for the first fitting variant (stage 02 on); (5) the yard is reached from the Mount's menu only after the Mount is done ("and later from any chapter": the same button, once the chapters exist).
- **Deviations:** (1) the vision's act is a practice run of the laver on a copy of the Phase B save (its rating is not kept); its sunrise is the dawn light set over the practice's own sky; (2) the yard's grades count toward the families' levels (as the roles book's practice does in Phase B); the test's do not; (3) the demonstration plays the job by the same pointer surfaces an autoplayer uses; (4) the test also opens right after the vision (not only when the kid asks for stage 02), so the gate is met in one flow; (5) "נגר" (התאמה) is listed as "בקרוב": no fitting variant exists before stage 02.
- **How out/index.html was made:** `node build.mjs` then `node tools/mkout.cjs "בנייה 44 — חזון, חצר האומנים ומבחן — בית המקדש השני" --no-shift` (no head script: Phase A opens).
- **Rule lapses:** none.

### build 45 — stage 02 "חומת העזרה", the new way (27.10; the coordinator's build-45 brief; docs/specs/shlav-alef-v2-he.md §7, §10 "02", §2 the pulley, §3 the ashlar, the giant, the corner, the retreat)
- **What:** (1) **One director for both stages**: `src/game/har42.js` `createHar42({ stage: 'wall' })` — `STG`, its own progress in the save (`bh_har42.wall`: part, open, grades, hands, the מהודר streak), the families' levels (lvl36) and the materials by day (MAT42) shared with stage 01; `PARTS45` = open → line → pulley → wcourse0 → wcourse1 → giant → wcorner → top → reveal. Taken over in Phase A right after the craftsman's test (and at Phase A's start when the Mount is done and the wall is not) — `game/index.js` `wanted45` / `startHar45`; the old flow behind the same switches (`?wall45=0`, `?stick=1`, `?har42=0`, the demo, an old-way wall already begun); the stages after it play as before. Developers' fixture `?h45go=1` (the Mount done, the vision seen, the test passed). (2) **The opening**: over the finished plaza the court wall's gold plan rises from the trench (187 × 135 inside, on the wall's line — מדות ה, א), the wall's crews already at the dig; 8 s the first time, later 3 s, the skip from the 3rd second. (3) **The work front**: north of the court's north wall (outer face z −73.5), its courses 28.5 amot out with the wall and its scaffold beyond them, every camera from the north (the follow camera's azimuth turned for this stage). Measured (the runner's new `--occ`: the meshes between the camera and him at every shot): nothing between them in any act or walk. Two layouts failed first: at the old stage's work spot ('dress', 3 amot from a face) the walks' camera stood inside the wall; inside the court the floor rises with the wall (`stages.groundAt`, up to 16 amot) and buried the course. (4) **The LINE** (`fam42` `createLine45`, `har42` `mkLine`): a drag from the near pin to the far one, graded by straightness (4 % / 9 % of its length times the level's tolerance; level 1 forgives a finger, 1.5); a let-go midway — the line falls slack, a correction (−0.2); a tap never stretches it; "וקו ינטה על ירושלים" (זכריה א, טז; the chalk line itself המחשה); at level 1 the guide dashed. (5) **The PULLEY**, hand over hand (`createPulley`, `mkPulley`): short downward swipes, each raises the stone a little; a still hold between pulls catches the rope, else it slips back; stop in the window (its shadow at level 1); past it a correction, left alone it is let out — never stuck; the meter beside the rig. (6) **PLACE**: wcourse0/1 — his stretch of 2–3 hand stones (2/3/4, "אריח על גבי לבנה", מגילה טז ע"ב), the crews lay the rest one by one, the pull-back at each course's end; **the settle fixed**: a released stone settles by itself (a 0.22-s drop, the thud event, the haptic) — no hold; precision still counts (released > 1.6 units from where it lands: imprecise, every second one a fault) and so do the joints; the drop read in the course's own units as projected (perspective — the screen's straight line misread the top course by 3 units); the stones that fit no free place dimmed; a refused stone keeps the verb ("גררו אבן קצרה יותר") and the course's line comes back in 2.6 s. **The giant** low in the wall — the crowbar flick reused (6 amot). **The corner course**: the corner stone the other way from the one below (the corners alternate course to course — המחשה), the pile's "┐ פינה" / "┌ פינה", the wrong way a fault, never corrected. **The top course** entirely his — lengths, the corner, and at level 3 the retreat: the plumb shows the stones that stand out, a drag pushes each back. Three מהודר in a row → "יישר כח!" (the crew, טיוטה). The court wall's own model rises with the graded parts. (7) **Rain at night** (תענית כג ע"א): a morning a caravan came in stage 02 opens wet — puddles, the stones and the paving darker (`stages.wet`) — and dries in ≈ 70 s; no gameplay (`?h45wet=1` for developers). (8) **ONE card**: "חומת העזרה — נבנתה", the ratings, the hands-on minutes, "נפתח: שערי העזרה", the five sources. Materials: the wall costs 32 (pulley 4, three wall courses 5 each, giant 5, top 8) — beyond a morning's caravan and stage 01's leftover, so "מחר: …" appears in normal play. (9) **The yard** gains the pulley, the line and the corner course. Lines that said "הניפו" / "דחפו" now say "גררו" (the naive kid stood at them; the crowbar's overshoot line is shared with stage 01).
- **Numbers:** **950 tests (940 pass, 10 skipped, 0 fail)** — `test/build45.test.mjs` 7 (taps alone never finish the pulley, the line or a course — a hundred taps of every kind; the settle: no hold, precision still counted, the drop read as projected; the corner rule: alternating by course, the wrong way a fault, the retreat at level 3; the pulley's catch, slip, window and let-out; the line's straightness, its let-go, its levels; the stage: its parts each with its own entry, its lines and sources, its materials beyond a morning, its wiring, the work front, the yard's new variants, GAME_BUILD 45, the Name scan; the K45 stall fixes); the older pins moved with comments (build42 'unsettled' → 'ok'; build43 four regexes; build44 the yard's list); **qa 12/12**; **dist 5,747,663 B (5.48 MiB) ≤ 6 MiB**; out/index.html 5,748,264 B. **The autoplayer through stage 02** (`?h45go=1&h42stock=99`): 640 × 296 (a45-P2) 137 s, 7 grades — 5 מהודר, 2 כשר; 844 × 390 (a45-W3, the final page) 135 s, 5 מהודר, 2 כשר, his hands 47 s; 0 page errors. **The naive kid from stage 02's start** (the final page and harness, 9 min budget): 640 × 296 (K45-P5) — the wall done at **2:48**, **0 stucks, no parent's hand**, hands-on 1.7 min, level-1 מהודר 2 of 4 (line מהודר, pulley כשר, the two courses כשר / מהודר; giant, corner, top at level 2: כשר, טעון תיקון, טעון תיקון), 0 page errors; 844 × 390 (K45-W5) — the wall done at **3:53**, **0 stucks, no parent's hand**, hands-on 2.8 min, level-1 מהודר 2 of 3 (line מהודר, pulley טעון תיקון, the first course מהודר; the rest at level 2: four כשר), 0 page errors. On the way (each run's stall fixed before the next): K45-P / K45-W (before the fixes) 5 and 3 parent's hands, never finished — a refused stone's line ("בחרו קצרה יותר") was no task to him; K45-P2 / W2: 0 and 1 (the crowbar's "דחפו"), done 8:30 / 6:08; K45-P3 / W3: 1 and 1 (his drags on the giant fell outside the half-screen pad), done 5:45 / 5:07; K45-P4 / W4: 1 and 2 (the harness: "בעדינות" read as the pour's "עד", a pile of one bright stone taken for the roller), done 5:20 / 7:21.
- **Verified by:** node tests; through `brun` only, every browser job in the background: the autoplayer a45-dev1…dev7 (the layouts, with the new `--occ`), a45-P, a45-W, a45-S, a45-P2, a45-W3 (the final page), a45-S2/S3 (the opening); the naive kid K45-P…P5, K45-W…W5. PNGs opened (`/tmp/claude-0/-home-claude/92bf52df-7cb5-51be-8ceb-ea3690eaf336/auto/`): `a45-W3/shot-open-plan.png` (the opening: the finished plaza, the court's dig and its gold plan risen on the wall's line, the crews), `a45-W3/shot-act-line.png` (the line from his pin to the far one, the guide dashed, "וקו ינטה על ירושלים"), `a45-W3/shot-act-pulley.png` (the gin pole, its stone and rope, the meter with its window), `a45-W3/shot-act-wcorner.png` (the corner course over two courses, the corner's place outlined, "┐ פינה" / "┌ פינה" in the pile, the wall's scaffold beyond), `a45-W3/shot-card.png` (the one card: 5 מהודר · 2 כשר, hands 1 min, "נפתח: שערי העזרה", the five sources); also the giant from its east, the top course, the reveal from the north, the wet morning (a45-W/a45-S).
- **What remains:** (1) the top course's camera looks along the course from its east end (pan40 frames all 15 units with him); readable, not broadside; (2) the naive kid's grades at the courses are mostly כשר — he ignores the red joints at level 1 (the rule's teacher) as a child would; (3) the rain is a touch of the stage-02 mornings only (no rain at night drawn — the morning after); (4) the opening is 8 s of game time — headless it ran ≈ 20 s at 844 (the frame rate); (5) the court wall's crews are the old stage's crews (crews.js) — they lay the wall's model as the parts are graded, not stone by stone in step with his courses.
- **Deviations:** (1) the work front stands outside the court, north of its north wall (inside, the floor rises with the wall) — his courses are a stretch of the wall's work in front of it, not its own stones; (2) every measurement ran with `?h42stock=99` (materials never short) — the materials by day are those of stage 01 (32 for the wall, so a second morning's caravan and "מחר: …" in normal play), tested in node; (3) the line's level-1 tolerance is 1.5 (a finger's wobble), the levels narrow it to 0.8; (4) the giant's line and the crowbar's overshoot line (shared with stage 01) now say "גררו את המוט"; the course's line no longer says "והחזיקו רגע" (stage 01 too); (5) three shared changes reach stage 01: the settle without hold, the drop read as projected, the dimmed stones that fit nowhere.
- **How out/index.html was made:** `node build.mjs` then `node tools/mkout.cjs "בנייה 45 — חומת העזרה — בית המקדש השני" --no-shift` (no head script: Phase A opens).
- **Rule lapses:** (1) one Monitor watch was written with a shell `&` (a `tail -f` in the background inside its script, killed by the script itself at the job's end); (2) a queued browser job was stopped mid-run: a45-W2's `brun` was launched without a longer `timeout`, waited 27 minutes in the queue and was cut by the 30-minute limit of its background task at the stage's reveal (its shots up to there kept; the 844 measurement rerun as a45-W3 with a long timeout); (3) two of my own waiting loops (not queue jobs) were stopped with TaskStop after that.

### build 46 — his stones ARE the court wall; stage 03 "הסורג והחיל" the new way (27.10; the coordinator's build-46 brief; docs/specs/shlav-alef-v2-he.md §1, §10 "03", §3 the lattice)
- **What:** **A — stage 02, his stones are the wall** (v2 §1, "שלי"): his hand stones, the corner, the top course, the giant and the crews' stones of his stretch are now the courses of a real stretch of the court wall's north face (x −111…−87, the stones' faces at z −73.54), laid stone by stone; the stretch stands over the Chel's terrace to come (y 6–11 — stage 03 lays the Chel 6 amot high round the courts; at the wall's foot his stones would be buried). The model's outer skin there and the scaffold in front of it are not drawn (`batch.js` `XRAY.uHole/uHole2/uHole3` in the shared shader patch; the scaffold's wood patched too). The stretch is kept in the save as laid (`bh_har42.wall.stones`: w, h, d, x, y, z, his — after each part and at the reveal), rebuilt on a later visit to a stage begun, and stands for good once the wall is done (`har42.js` `wallStones45`, `game/index.js` `ensureWall45` at Phase A's start). When he passes within 18 amot (no stage on), his own stones glow faintly and "האבנים שלך" (טיוטה) shows — once a minute at most. He works from the ground 5 amot out; the giant waits on the ground until its part; the corner and the top course framed from the corner's east end (camera north, broadside for the corner; the top still from its east end). **B — stage 03** (`createHar42({ stage: 'soreg' })`, `PARTS46` = open → lattice0 → lattice1 → lattice2 → warn → bows → reveal; taken over right after the wall in v2's order — `wanted46` / `startHar46`, the wall's card now says "נפתח: הסורג והחיל"; the old flow behind `?soreg46=0` / `?stick=1` / `?har42=0`; the fixture `?h46go=1`): (1) **Opening**: over the finished court wall, the soreg's gold line rises round the courts (−211…165 × ±91.5, temple.js), the crews already at the Chel (its model rises with his graded parts); 8 s, later 3 s and a skip. (2) **The LATTICE** (`fam42` `createLattice46`, `har42` `mkLattice`): three panels of the north soreg (10 טפחים, never a high barrier), each with a gap; the panel's pattern on a board over the scene, the panel before it at the left (the pattern runs on across the panels); the tray's three pieces — a tap turns, a double tap flips, a drag sets it in the gap; the right shape the right way goes in, anything else comes out again and counts a mistake (no auto-correct); graded by the mistakes and the turns beyond the fewest; diamonds → interlaced (over/under) → two tones (a tick that shows the hand); the world's panel drawn from the same pattern; the model's own soreg not drawn where his panels stand (`uHoleB`). (3) **The WARNING STONES** (`createSpacing46`, `mkWarn`): held anywhere, he walks along the soreg (the side he holds — the way he goes; at a post he turns back), "הציבו כאן" sets a stone with the Greek inscription's opening words where he stands (3, at level 3: 4); a stone within 1.5 amot of a post or another is refused; after the last, the real gaps in gold with their lengths and the even places as faint stones; within 8 % מהודר, 18 % כשר (the levels tighten it); source chip יוספוס, מלחמות ה 193–194 (the placement המחשה). (4) **The THIRTEEN PROSTRATIONS** (`mkBows`): a quiet ~25-s moment, not a skill — a procession of six priests along the soreg between him and it, thirteen gold marks; he does the first with a slow swipe down (40 px over ¼ s, anywhere on the screen; after 14 s it begins by itself), then the procession bows at each mark in its own rhythm and he may bow with it; the line "משתחווים מול המקדש — הודיה על הניצחון על היוונים" with "מדות ב, ג" and "ברטנורא שם" (טיוטה); no grade, no haptics, no jingle. (5) **One card**: the ratings, the hands-on minutes, "ממצא: כתובת האזהרה ביוונית — נמצאה ב-1871" (the game's own find, 'warning'), the bows he joined, "נפתח: שערי העזרה", the sources. Materials: the soreg costs 30 (three panels 8, the stones 6) — a second morning's caravan in normal play. The yard gains the lattice (its panel by the level) and the spacing. Cameras: the lattice front-on (ש3) from the north, the walk along the soreg from the north, the procession low along the soreg; every one at his ≤ 45 % (`needD`).
- **Numbers:** **955 tests (945 pass, 10 skipped, 0 fail)** — `test/build46.test.mjs` 5 (taps alone never finish a lattice or the spacing — a hundred taps and double taps, a drag off the gap, "set" without walking; the pattern rule: orientations, the run across panels, two tones, the right shape the wrong way a mistake, the fewest turns מהודר, the levels; the spacing's grades and refusals; his stones persist — `wallStones45` from a saved list, one stretch never two, the holes' uniforms, the shader's and the scaffold's patch, the stretch in the face over the Chel, kept at the reveal, his marks, "האבנים שלך", `ensureWall45`; the stage — parts, lines, sources, the 1871 find, no haptics and no grade in the bows, the full-screen walk and bows, the post's stop, the wiring, the yard, GAME_BUILD 46, the Name scan); the older pins moved with comments (GAME_BUILD in builds 32–45; build43 the card's sources; build44 the yard's list; build45 the director's STG, the work front at z −100, the yard's slice); **qa 12/12**; **dist 5,774,158 B (5.51 MiB) ≤ 6 MiB**; out/index.html 5,774,750 B. **The autoplayer** — through both stages (a46-P 640 / a46-W 844, `?h45go=1`, `--after`): stage 02 → its card ("נפתח: הסורג והחיל") → stage 03 → its card, 0 page errors; stage 03 on the final page (`?h46go=1`): 640 × 296 (a46-P2) 190 s, 3 מהודר + 1 כשר; 844 × 390 (a46-W2) 215 s, 4 מהודר; 0 page errors. **The naive kid from stage 03's start** (the final page, 9-min budget): 640 × 296 (K46-P4) — the soreg done at **2:53**, **0 stucks, no parent's hand**, hands-on 1.5 min, level-1 מהודר 0 of 4 (lattices כשר, כשר, טעון תיקון; the stones טעון תיקון), bowed with the procession 5 times; 844 × 390 (K46-W4) — done at **4:28**, 0 stucks, no parent's hand, hands-on 3.1 min, level-1 מהודר 1 of 4, bowed 7 times; 0 page errors. On the way: K46-P / K46-W never reached the bows (the walk's surface was off — only the bot's synthetic events reached the pad: 6 min holding the walk layer), K46-P2 / W2 done at 8:56 / 5:33 but the stones took 5 min (he held toward the post) and no bow joined, K46-P3 / W3 done 6:14 / 7:29, still no bow joined — K46-dbg (a 2.5-min run at the bows with a temporary log) showed his swipes never reached the pad: touch40 had boxed it round the thing; the walk and the bows now take the whole screen. **Stage 02 with A** (the naive kid K46-S, 640, `?h45go=1`, 9 min): the wall done, its stretch kept — 36 stones in the save, 16 of them his — then on into stage 03 (its lattices done); 1 parent's hand (the crowbar's overshoot, as in build 45), 0 page errors.
- **Verified by:** node tests; through `brun` only, every browser job in the background with the long timeout: the autoplayer a46-dev1/dev2 (stage 02's stretch), a46-s1/s2 (stage 03), a46-P, a46-W (both stages), a46-S (both, the shots and a hand-set last look at his stretch), a46-S3, a46-P2, a46-W2 (stage 03, the final page); the naive kid K46-P…P4, K46-W…W4, K46-S, K46-dbg. PNGs opened (`/tmp/claude-0/-home-claude/92bf52df-7cb5-51be-8ceb-ea3690eaf336/auto/`): `a46-S/shot-look.png` (after both stages: his stretch standing in the court wall's north face over the Chel — drafted ashlars, the giant at its foot, the rest of the wall the model's), `a46-S/shot-act-lattice2.png` (the two-tone panel on its board, the panel before it at the left, the gap dashed, the three pieces; the low soreg below), `a46-S/shot-warn-result.png` (the real gaps in gold — 7.7 · 6.3 · 8.1 · 6.3 · 7.6 — the largest miss 13 %), `a46-S/shot-bow.png` (the procession of priests between him and the soreg, the gold marks, the line and its chips), `a46-S3/shot-card-soreg.png` (the one card: the ratings, the hands, the 1871 find, the bows joined, "נפתח: שערי העזרה", the five sources); also the act shots of stage 02's courses in the wall (`a46-S/shot-act-wcorner.png`).
- **What remains:** (1) the naive kid's lattices are mostly טעון תיקון at level 1 — he picks and turns at random, as a logic puzzle allows; the board shows the pattern round the gap and the panel before, no auto-correct; (2) the faint glow and "האבנים שלך" were seen only in the hand-set look (no walking run past the stretch after stage 03); (3) the Chel's own model rises with his graded parts, not stone by stone; the crews at the Chel are the old stage's crews; (4) the top course's camera still looks along it from its east end; (5) the soreg's thirteen marks stand only at the bows' stretch (the procession's thirteen), not round the whole soreg.
- **Deviations:** (1) his stretch stands over the Chel's terrace (y 6–11) — at the wall's foot it would be buried by the 6-amah Chel stage 03 lays — and he works it from the ground 5 amot out; (2) the lattice is solved on a board over the scene (front-on, so the pattern reads at a phone's size); the world's panel shows the same pattern and fills when he is right; (3) every measurement ran with `?h42stock=99`; the soreg's materials (30) tested in node; (4) the bows' first begins by itself after 14 s (a quiet moment never waits on him); (5) the wall's card now names stage 03 next ("נפתח: הסורג והחיל"), and stage 03 follows the wall at once when played the new way; (6) the shared shader patch (batch.js) gains two hole boxes (his stretch, his panels); the scaffold's wood material is patched too.
- **How out/index.html was made:** `node build.mjs` then `node tools/mkout.cjs "בנייה 46 — הסורג — בית המקדש השני" --no-shift` (no head script: Phase A opens).
- **Rule lapses:** none (every queued browser job had the long timeout; no queue job stopped; no shell `&`).

### recovery (22.10.2026)
- **What was lost.** On the night of 21.10.2026 (the owner's first reaction is logged at 23:54, Asia/Jerusalem) the cloud computer the work ran on was replaced and `/home/claude/temple3d` — the code of builds 16–26c, the documents written since the 16.10 hand-off, the tests added after build 15 (144, so 568 → 712), the autoplayer (`tools/autoplay35.js` + `autoplay35-run.cjs`), PROJECT.md's entries for 16–24, `docs/specs`, `docs/review-18-10`, `docs/review-19-10`, `docs/stations-19-10`, HANDOFF's §5א-1 / §5ב and the rule-3 change, OWNER-NOTES since 18.10 — was gone. GitHub (`yaronbo-cmd/mikdash`, branch main; Pages at `https://yaronbo-cmd.github.io/mikdash/`) held only build 15 — two commits, both 16.10: "Add GitHub Pages deploy workflow" (e657abe) and "Initial import: source of build 15 + built index.html for GitHub Pages" (9d6e176); the owner had said (21.10 00:17) that his app pushed build 22 — what the session saw on GitHub was build 15 only. What was left: the owner's `Desktop\mikdash` — `index.html` of **build 26c** (the one full copy of the code of 16–26c, as a minified bundle: 5,417,820 B, sha256 `b1183ea9fd0ad04de20add2aa02a5068eb7a9dc70b243d9958bec02fb8d9adae`, title "משמרת 45 (בנייה 26ג) — בית המקדש השני") and `mikdash-src-build15.zip` (the build-15 source) —, his attachments (re-attached to the session) and the session's own transcript (the summary of builds 17a–24, the full reports of 25–26c, and the Bash/Write calls that wrote the specs). The owner approved the restore from the bundle ("מאשר", 21.10 23:55).
- **How it was recovered (R1–R10; details and numbers in `recovery/R1-map.md`, status per module in `recovery/status.json`).** **R1** — the map: the 26c bundle was made by the same esbuild (0.28.x) with build.mjs's settings, and `main.js`'s `await import(...)` makes esbuild wrap almost every module in a lazy wrapper, so the bundle cuts itself into modules with exact boundaries (179 segments); of build 15's 225 bundled files 200 were unchanged in 26c, 25 changed, none gone; **12 modules of 26c had no build-15 source** — builds 16–26c's code (focus17, fx18, ramp19, kiyor20, deshen21, maaracha22, pis23, pen25, heichal26, the event log, the gait table, and 4 bytes of glue); `brun` (the browser queue: one job at a time, cut at 10 minutes) recreated at `/home/claude/wt/bus/brun` (source in `recovery/tools/brun.sh`), Chromium through Playwright verified. **R2** — **the parity tool** (`recovery/tools/parity.mjs`): a module is right when its minified units have the same shapes as 26c's AND the bound names map as one consistent alpha-renaming bijection over the whole module AND every name it uses from another module comes from the same module (it passes its own self-test: three mutations the earlier metric could not see all FAIL). **R3–R5** — the small and mid-size ports by parity (temple, ui, route31, world22 + walk, sound, audio, twin, hero35, camera, flow31, hud, stations …), each `PASS` before its commit. **R6** — the nine new day modules (focus17 … heichal26) with `unmin.mjs`, which un-minifies a 26c draft by a names file (every rename scope-checked) — PASS in stand-in trees; **R7** — day33.js (generated from the 26c draft; the original build-26 lines quoted in the transcript supplied names and comments), shift/index.js, lobby35; **R8** — the page's names. **Result: `parity --all` = 280 modules, 280 PASS** (src/ 236); **the page** built from the tree has the same length as 26c's (5,417,820 B) and **the same tokens, but is not byte-identical: 1,520 of 1,751,154 tokens are names, in 10 minified-name pairs** (esbuild names symbols by source-level use counts, which the lost comments and folded locals leave no trace of: three local-slot near-ties, three top-level names in pis23, one unused constant in heichal26); `node build.mjs` gives `dist/temple3d.html` 5,417,106 B — the size build 26c's own report gave. **Screenshots side by side** (the restored page and 26c's, through `brun`, a returning player's save): the start screen, day 1's laver as he walks up to the spigots, the hold pose with both rings — the same, with the same engine state and HUD text and no errors. **R9** — the tests: the five build-15 tests that 26c's behaviour no longer met were updated, each with a comment (shift30's pyre stand; day33's ramp act, the washed logs' grades, the next station, the card at the top), and tests for builds 16–26c written from the behaviour in the reports (`test/build16 … build26c.test.mjs`, 146 tests, on `test/rig33.mjs`; the suite 714: 704 pass, 10 skipped, 0 fail), the 6 MiB cap in build.mjs and qa-content-scan, `tools/mkout.cjs`. **R10** — the documents: `docs/specs` (replayed from the transcript's own write calls where it had them, from the owner's attachments and the summary where not), this file, HANDOFF, OWNER-NOTES.
- **Lost for good.** (a) **Every comment of builds 16–26c in the code** (the bundle keeps none; the recovered files carry `recovered` comments written from the bundle, the reports and ≈ 30 original lines the transcript quotes) and every local name the reports do not give (the recovery's names are marked in each file's header; 10 name pairs differ from the page). (b) **The original tests of builds 16–26c** — 144 tests, 568 → 712 — and **the original autoplayer** (`tools/autoplay35.js` + `autoplay35-run.cjs`): R9's tests are new, written for the behaviour, and R9 re-created the autoplayer (the two files carry the header `recovered (R9)`); the autoplayer's numbers in the reports above (the morning's seconds, mastery, fire, coins) were measured by the ORIGINAL tool and are history, not a baseline the re-created one is bound to match. (c) The documents `docs/review-18-10`, `docs/review-19-10`, `docs/stations-19-10` (docx copies on the owner's Desktop: `chavat-daat-yod-18-10-2026.docx`, `chavat-daat-yod-alef-19-10-2026.docx`, `dapei-tachanot-19-10-2026.docx`, and `prompt-gemini-tmura-he.txt`, `she-ela-mumche-pis-he.txt`). (d) **Claude's original notes on the older specs**, the input protocol's camera text and conflict list, `pis-boker`'s expert answer, and the Levites' song / ramp-run / salting texts (each spec file says what it is and what was rebuilt). (e) PROJECT.md's original entries for 16–24, rewritten above. (f) The scratch files of the builds (PNGs, autoplayer logs, `mkout` scripts per build).
- **The backup rule (from 22.10).** With EVERY build a full source zip goes to the owner's `Desktop\mikdash` (named like the build-15 one — `mikdash-src-build<N>.zip`; the tree without `node_modules/`, `dist/` and `out/`: `src/`, `content/`, `assets/`, `legacy/`, `test/`, `tools/`, `docs/`, `recovery/`, `build.mjs`, `package.json`, `package-lock.json`, `PROJECT.md`) beside `index.html`, through the connected computer's file tool; Claude's promise to the owner, 21.10: "מהבנייה הבאה, בכל מסירה אשמור בתיקייה mikdash אצלך גם עותק מלא של הקוד, כדי שזה לא יחזור". The session NEVER pushes (the owner: "שכח פה מהדחיפה לגיט האב. תתעלם פה מההוראה הזו. הסתדרתי כבר מהאפליקציה") — local commits only; connecting the repo to Claude's sessions, so Claude can keep a remote backup itself, is a later step with Nachshon. In a new session the first command is `git log --oneline | head`, and the second is a check that the tree is the tip.
- **State after the recovery, and the queue.** The tree builds, the page equals 26c up to the ten name pairs, the suite is green. Known superseding: HANDOFF rule 2 (blood may be graphic, the owner 18.10) overrides §2.9 above; rule 3 (the Heichal yes, the Holy of Holies no — the owner 21.10 08:25: "כן. להתיר כניסה להיכל.") overrides the earlier "never enters" for the Heichal only (§2.5 stands for the Holy of Holies). The queue, one build at a time ("כל פעם בצע משימה אחת בלבד. התקדמות הדרגתית."), by `docs/specs/seder-haboker-he.md`: the slaughter and the receiving of the blood (`shchita-kabala-he.md`, `zerikat-dam-he.md`), the limbs' procession (`nesiat-evarim-he.md`) and raising the limbs (`haalaat-evarim-he.md`), the flaying cutscene (`hafshet-nituach-he.md`), the oil and chavitin (`chavitin-he.md`), the flour offerings (`minchot-shtayim-he.md`), the wine (`nisuch-hayayin-he.md`) with the Levites' song (`shirat-halevi-im-he.md`; the kohen leads with the silver trumpets), the incense (`ketoret-he.md`), the priestly blessing (`birkat-kohanim-he.md`; the third input exception), the Shema (`heleni-shema-he.md`), the mastery ladder (`dargot-meyumanut-he.md`); the open questions in 26b/26c above (the child's pace on the menorah, the phone's bass, the song's loop). Decisions log: `docs/specs/hachraot-he.md`.
